Salma gets up to pour herself another glass of cider, fills half a glass, raises it to her lips and looks at some photos on the shelf, she keeps silent, there are photos of Roland as a child with his parents. She turns her head, frowning, as if something is on her mind.

― Sal: EEYYY!!! -She screams scaring everyone-. The photos!!!!

― Rol: What’s wrong with the photos? -he asks, and at that very moment his surprised expression changes-. The photos!!! We’ve forgotten them!

― Sal: The suits, where are they?! -she asks heading towards the corridor leading to the rooms. Nikoleta and Lázaro come out behind her.

Ω

That Whole Naga Thing

Several days later. Nikoleta and Lázaro are walking with Salma, they advance along a greenway, an old railway line that crosses between mountains, a few steps away is the abutment of the seven moons’ bridge, at the top “la font rotja” (the red fountain) appears between the pine forests, in front of them opens the way “el barranc de l’infern” (the ravine of hell) and to the right the Mariola’s mountain range. It is a sunny morning but with fog banks, Alcoi looks like a city that dawns from the past.

― Lázaro: I need to stop… me moric! (I’m dying!)

― Nikoleta: -she looks at him with a dismissive gesture- Really Willy? You’re so delicate.

― Láz: Wow! Auntie, you’ve rested twenty times and I haven’t said anything.

― Nik: I know, but it’s just that we’re going very quiet and I’m bored…

― Láz: So buy a monkey and talk to him.

― Nik: And why do you think I’m paying you, HAAA!

― Láz: Ayyyyyy! Salma, tell her to leave me alone -he says, wrinkling his face.

― Sal: Do I look like a mother to you? You can ask my sister or Roland for that. Don’t bother me, I’m leaving you here -she says, sitting down on the ground with her back against the railing, she takes out the water and drinks. Lázaro sits with her while Nikoleta looks out over the ravine.

― Láz: Salma, can I ask you something?

― Sal: No water, no money. Nor that I throw Nikoleta over the bridge -Nikoleta turns around with a one-eyed smile.

― Láz: Seriously, we don’t get the whole Naga thing, could you explain it to us?

― Sal: But there’s no one who understands it, it doesn’t matter if I try to explain it to you or Roland.-both look at her nodding, eager for her explanation-. OK, from the beginning, I’ll do it outline-style and with drawings, which is always more didactic -she says, laughing as she takes a pen and a crumpled piece of paper out of her backpack.

Let’s see…

Who are the Naga?

They are aliens from another corner of the universe.

Why do they come to earth?

They came to exploit their resources, as they had already ran out of their resources on their own planet.

So they are conquerors?

Yes, conquerors from other worlds, and they also take advantage of the existing life on the planet they arrive on in order to obtain labour. They are slavers. If the life form is capable of meeting their needs, they enslave it. If not, they create new life forms.

How do they create new forms of life?

Naga technology is highly advanced. They take genes from animals native to the planet, as they are adapted to it. In the Earth’s specific case, these were primates. The genes they take from the animal are crossed with the Naga’s own genes, genes that have also been purified from Teraghe`n. This mixture of genes results in humanoid-shaped animals, with a minimal intelligence that allows them to be easily exploited without rebellion.

What does it mean to purge the genes of Teraghe`n?

The Naga are able to control the universal flux, known as Teraghe-n- Magheq, the flux that allows them to have their abilities. When the Naga want to create creatures of low evolutionary level, they remove the Teraghe`n mark from their genes. The genetic information that allows them to sense and use Teraghe`n does not exist in humans.

Who are the Tinddaya?

We Tinddaya are the same gene-crossing as the Yamnaya, but with unpurified Naga genes. So we have the ability to manipulate Teraghe`n, although to a lesser extent than the Naga.

Why do the Tinddaya and Yamnaya exist?

The Naga destroyed their own civilisation and turned the planet into a barren rock as it was charred by bombs. There were Nagas who were off-planet during the final war, and that gave them some chance of survival.

Eight Naga were stranded on planet Earth and in their flight, their ship was the only refuge. Doomed to extinction, they had the idea of prolonging their genes by creating the Tinddaya. Now, on Earth, there were two races, one destined to thrive over the other.

How many Naga are there?

There were originally eight Naga on the planet, Alaris, Shamkra Baem, Gala Évora, Niorai, Ràdamus, Cräpatos, Dhyavod and Moor. It is said that from their conjunction the ninth Naga arose, which would give rise to a ninth lineage. Moor was the most fractious of the Naga, known to have been isolated. Moor, in a rage, instilled in the necromancers, and later in the Yamnaya, a hatred for the rest of the Tinddaya.

The descendants of Alaris are the architects, of Shamkra Baem the combatants, of Gala Évora the physicians, of Niorai the mentalists, of Ràdamus the alchemists, of Cräpatos the travellers, of Dhyavod the engineers, of Moor the necromancers, and of the conjunction, the sensibles’ lineage.

Another Naga creations?

To guard the Tinddaya, the Naga gave rise to guardians, creatures designed to maintain the status quo. These creatures inhabit the world of dreams.

The guardians were accompanied by seven masks, which are the only way to consciously enter the dream world.

The guardians are Sköll for Alaris, Garuda for Shamkra Baem, Shenron for Gala Évora, Aranyani for Niorai, Bamur ku for Ràdamus, Tanuk ik for Cräpatos and Oren for Dhyavod.

Moor created his own guardians, whom he called war masters. They are Buri Buri for Sköll, Titan for Garuda, Kuagssak for Shenron, Ion for Aranyani, Diersej for Bamur ku, Kennin ji for Tanuk ik and Faust for Oren. There is no mask for Moor.

What is the dream plane?

To maintain order, the Naga generate a mental fabric capable of connecting all Yamnaya, and by which they are able to spread orders on a mass level. These orders are executed by the guardians, and altered by the warlords.

How do the Tinddaya and Yamnaya evolve?

Originally, the Tinddaya kept the Yamnaya as slaves. But in the course of time there was a Yamnaya uprising, which led to war. After the war there was a harmonisation, Tinddaya crossed with Yamnaya, which is why today a Tinddaya can be born from a Yamnaya. This situation gave rise to the 3rd City, a place where the two species coexisted.

Some time later the necromancers started another war in order to return the Tinddaya to their place of dominance over the Yamnaya.

What does each lineage do?

The architects are the ones who build all the materials, build languages, systems, etc., and upon these elements, the engineers generate their own inventions. The mentalists can read the mind, understand it, manipulate it, and the sensibles are the cornerstone, they have super senses, they can speak to the essence of things and they are the ones who understand Teraghe`n best. Then there are the galenos, who are the Tinddaya sanitarians. The combatants are there to keep the “peace” and maintain the “balance”, they can override the other lineages.

The alchemists and the necromancers, some walk the light, others the shadows, it is assumed that in their beginnings they went hand in hand, they needed each other to work, but the schism produced by the wars with the Yamnaya resulted in the absolute separation. And finally the travellers, who can travel in time and space, and those of high level can manipulate them.

Who are the necromancers?

At present we know that there is a markedly belligerent group, the Ravens. They have a leadership that directs them, and hundreds of them under their command. They have been known since the Italian renaissance.

Their powers come from the plane of shadows. They live by death and manipulate it. They kill without mercy.

They also seek to break the masquerade, they want the Tinddaya to be recognised over the Yamnaya.

Necromancers are treacherous and jealous of each other. Among the ravens themselves, blood crimes for the sake of power are commonplace.

The necromancers derogatorily call the Yamnaya “steppe people”, it is their derogatory way of calling them. The term “human” is of Yamnaya origin, they created it to separate themselves from the Tinddaya.

Who are the Antediluvians?

The Antediluvians, or the Wayota of the volcanoes, are necromancers operating in the present from the past. They are the most powerful Tinddaya active nowadays. Their lackeys or fayac are necromancers.

What is Teraghe`n?

Teraghe-n-Magheq is life, it is the universal flow, neither bad nor good. Good and evil are values that we cherish. Teraghe`n exists in a necromancer and in an alchemist, the difference is how it is used.

All Tinddaya have the ability to manipulate Teraghe`n, the Yamnaya are also surrounded by Teraghe`n, but cannot sense it. The ability is determined by our lineage and our level.

Teraghe`n can be quantified by the vibration we emit. The higher the amount, the higher the level, level 0 would be the highest level, and level 13 would be the lowest.

Mérida, Jimena and Alejo are level 0, you and I are level 2. The level is exponential, a level 1 would be 4 times more powerful than us.

The level is relevant, it determines the ability of the individual, and the more training, the more control and performance. But you can never go up a level.

And the symbols on the hands?

There is one circumference per lineage, each lineage has a variation in its pattern. The mestizos have two circumferences in which the lineage-specific shapes are intertwined.

Each person has his or her own circumference, it is like fingerprints, unique. It helps us to recognise each other among Tinddaya and between lineages. When you invoke them, they appear on the palm of your hand, and also when something important happens with the vital flow. It shines when we are born, when we die and when we use Teraghe`n at too high a level.

Mérida, Jimena and Alejo?

Mestizos are common, the rare thing is that they have level 0, and even more rare is that they have it in both lineages. The coincidence here is that 3 mestizos are born in the same place on the same day. This gave rise to a lot of speculation.

Jimena and Alejo are travellers and engineers. Mérida is a Necromancer and sensitive, it must be a drama to have a death wish and yet feel mercy and pain to a level that goes beyond madness… uff….

The three grew up together, learned together and were the only ones able to understand each other.

Where did we live?

We lived in Eritea, which is a hidden town in the south of Andalusia. In the summers there were holidays in the village of Nereo and Viveka. You don’t know exactly where their house is, you can only get there through bridging doors.

Nobody knows anything about us and our cities. It is better to stay hidden.

And what do we know for sure about ourselves?

The closest in time comes with the modern era, mainly the 15th century. You are familiar with Leonardo DaVinci, because he was an engineer and he shared a home with many Tinddaya. They lived in NeoArcadia, it is not known who founded it or where it was located.

In that century, the ravens, I Corvi, appeared and wrangled between Tinddaya and Yamnaya, giving rise to the Italian Wars. By 1559 Neo-Arcadia had fallen.

With the French and the industrial revolution there was a new attempt. The Yamnaya were offered our knowledge. Robespierre, a mentalist, defended equality and liberty and got to captivate the entire population, but failed to appreciate visions like Olympe de Gouges’ one, a sensitive, who ended up on the guillotine, like him shortly afterwards. The revolution was over in a matter of days.

After this, it was understood that coexistence was an absurdity. The best thing for us is to make ourselves invisible to history, to the Yamnaya, to become the Oblivium.

So much for what I know of our history. But if you still feel like it, even if I don’t, I’ll tell you a little about us before “the reign of the Empress” -the young couple nod.

― Láz: Quina colla, no? (what a gang, isn’t it?) It’s unbelievable that you can’t achieve peace being as clever as you are.

― Sal: Well… that’s the way it is. The first powers shoot each other with drones and in the Amazon they still shoot arrows. We come from creatures meaner than pain, what can I say?

― Nik: And the Tinddaya populations, when they manage to be at peace, do they work well?

― Sal: I guess. I’ve only known Eritea and Nereo’s Village and Viveka in depth, the rest of the towns I’ve just visited. I can only tell you about what I know.

What is Eritea?

It is the city where there was a revival of Tinddaya in the Western European area, it happened around 1931, it was a coming and going of Tinddaya from all over Europe and America. And it was the refuge where the 2nd World War and the Dictatorships went unnoticed.

Our city had everything you needed, education, industry, food. A beautiful place to grow up surrounded by fascinating people. We travelled by bridges to other places, there were no worries.

What about our childhood?

Our group was formed since childhood by Mérida, Jimena, Alejo, Roland, Nélida, Cornelia, Joan, Edisa and me, with our quarrels for inventing original things, with our outings, our parties, our annoyances, but always together.

Between Jimena, Alejo and Mérida, there was a tremendous synergy and a tremendous quarrel, especially between siblings. Jimena felt magnetism towards the shadow, and Alejo couldn’t stand Mérida, he only wanted his sister to build with him. Alejo unwittingly brought them closer together.

The inventions made by these three were unimaginable.

Over the years Mérida began to chart her own path, the path of shadows, and only them were aware of it. However, their thirst to know the boundaries between them was so great that they let her do her thing.

And what were our skills?

Roland was a photographer, portraying souls and capturing people. Nélida was capable of constructing musical boxes whose scores could alter any state of consciousness. I made fabrics capable of mimicking, disappearing and protecting.

Alejo invented books whose pages were written with thought, invisible inks that reacted to the owner’s touch, and the most amazing part was “God’s word”, but the wishes written in it only happen when the book wants them to, so he needed Chronos, the watch, to control the time of execution of those written wishes.

Jimena mastered a fork of time with respect to the present, to go back several days, to move it forward, to stop it. It gave rise to his masterpiece, “Chronos’ heart”. This invention was capable of stopping the body’s own time, immortality.

The siblings merged the two inventions into The Chronos Verses. An invention to manipulate history and time.

Mérida is a necromancer and a sensitive, which allows her to walk the shadows’ plane, move among the dead, summon demons, dominate through fear. As a sensitive, she experiences emotions in all their nuances, pain, love, when she comes into contact with another person or an object and even in a certain space.

As a sensitive person, Cornelia had the ability to communicate with animals, plants and stones. Together with Jimena they developed the translator “Neichur Transleichur”.

Joan, an engineer, perfected the bridge doors and invented drawn doors. Joan and Edisa are Alejandra’s parents. Edisa, a sensitive, only created a gadget, the television of possible futures, each of the channels is a possible future of our today.

The madness of Mérida?

The key point was when Mérida had access to the television, she spent days in front of it. I don’t know what she saw, but the next thing was to stage a coup d’état. She had known the future.

The kingdom of the Empress?

At the age of 26 she staged the coup, claiming to be Tinddaya’s Empress. She killed all who opposed her, and punished her detractors. Joan and Edisa opposed her outright and were annihilated. Roland, Nélida and I tried to attack her, and for this we were burned and locked up. Jimena and Alejo were in a state of shock, just watching her as she killed without doing anything. Jimena was locked in the tourmaline and Alejo was aged, both were the guarantee of her omnipotence. Cornelia managed to escape and hide. Mérida took Alejandra with her.

She took inventions from all of us, but her greatest piece was the Chronos Verses. Eternal life, absolute will, along with the worst of Mérida’s inventions, the fallacious rings. With them she would have control of all Tinddaya, total subjugation. To try to take them away was suicide.

And finally, how to communicate with her?

Before everything happened, Alejo, always suspicious, convinced the group to baptise us with a name that would allow us to recognise each other without the shadows being able to hear it. Koiki baptised us under the regency of light. Jimena is Mist Heron, Alejo is Fire Hawk, Roland is Stone Turtle, Nélida is Storm Deer, Cornelia is Elder Wolf, Edisa was Ivy Snake and Joan was Iron Bear, and I was Wind Fox. To refer to Mérida, we used Shadow Owl.

Nothing more to say, give me your water, you’ve got my mouth dry -she says, stretching out her arm towards Lázaro.

― Láz: You didn’t say that water is not to be shared -he laughs, taking the rucksack out of her reach.

― Sal: Give me the water or walk home -Lázaro and Nikoleta run ahead of Salma-. Really? -she takes the knob out of her pocket and shows it with a half-smile, walking in the opposite direction to the youngsters.

― Nik: Nooo! Don’t go away. Take the water! HAHAHAHAHAHAHAHA…

A Boomer Dragon in Modern Times

The Renaissance Club

Florence, 1498.

Bong, bong! The sound of the bells of the Talenti Tower startles the birds that take flight through the blue sky. Bong, bong! It chimes with a peculiar ring for those who know how to listen.

At the Palazzo Medici Riccardi the gates are open, passers-by stop to look curiously around the courtyard, there are a couple of zebras strolling around, paradise’s birds, hedgehogs and exotic plants. In the palace corridors, the rush of the waiters is obvious. Giuliano is sitting on one of the windowsills overlooking the garden, watching the animals, with his brother. Lorenzo is sitting in a purple velvet armchair with gold embroidery, mahogany wood, reading something that looks like a book but is not. Both are silent. Only the chirping of pigeons can be heard in a large inn in a vast, lavishly decorated room, bathed in the midday’s light.

― Giuliano: Do you like the multifunction artifact, brother? -Lorenzo nods without taking his eyes off the surface of the gadget.

― Lorenzo: It’s really fascinating. To be honest, it’s the best purchase I’ve ever made, I can read anything up until 2017 and choose from any genre, any language and any audience. Plus music. Holding more terabyte information than all the libraries in Europe altogether. At first I thought it would be the greatest foolishness ever, a digital warehouse with a moving image surface in exchange for the battle of the centaurs of my sculptor Michelangelo. Blessed foolishness.

― Giu: Will you let me use it? -he asks as he fiddles with a solar panel on the table next to the tablet charger.

― Lor: When you don’t see it in my hands, you can use it, the one who sacrificed a piece of the master was me. That traveller doesn’t mince his words when it comes to sending objects back in time. I can’t even imagine how he is able to do it under the ruling of the Empress. But honestly, I don’t care, after all, I shouldn’t be messing with the future either. But I have to treat myself for being the great protector of our beloved republic.

― Giu: You have a library full of 19th and 20th century books printed on paper. You could read one of those and let me have the gadget to listen to some music, I’ve also done my bit by obtaining a Viking speaker. My banner of Minerva and Cupid is a Botticelli that is well worth a couple of hours of daily use of the contraption.

― Lor: Next time we will order another one for you.

― Giu: You have placed two orders in less than a year. If the Prima Sangoli at the Dome discover our flirtations with the time, they will take everything away from us and amend us. We should not order any more, at least not until all this is over.

― Lor: We no longer exist for the world, brother. And I remain the man of power in this house until my son Pietro proves his worth. Honestly, I don’t know what the Dome is going to be upset about if I’m part of it. We wouldn’t be living in this moment if it weren’t for the flirtations with the future -Giuliano nods. Lorenzo continues reading.

― Giu: Is it interesting what you´re reading?

― Lor:Very much, it’s about four big-footed children, a dwarf, a man, a bushman and a sylvan, all led by a sorcerer who walks their world, looking for a treasure guarded by a incubus. Vigilantly the very eye of evil stalks them, sending its nine warlords to capture them. However, when I finish it, I will let you know -he pauses, pondering what he has just said.

― Giu: Here, play whatever music you want -he says, handing him the tablet. Giuliano turns on the Bluetooth and tinkers with the device. The music plays.

“Tu stupendo sei in amore

Sensuale sul mio cuore, sì

Se poi strappo un tuo lamento

è importante questo mio momento perché

Io ti chiedo ancora il tuo corpo ancora

Le tue braccia ancora

Di abbracciarmi ancora di amarmi ancora”

…

Giuliano sings the song with a gesture full of pleasure. Lorenzo continues with his serious countenance. He looks at his brother with attention.

― Lor: Lower your voice, please -Giuliano lowers the volume-. Brother.

― Giu: I’m listening -understanding by his tone that an important ponderation is coming.

― Lor: No empire, nor family is everlasting, only the steppes as a species seem to have that prerogative, or do you think NeoArcadia would survive without the Dome? Will it collapse like everything else collapses? Will our family, our species, the Prima Sangoli fall first?

― Giu: Brother, put those worries away. On the one hand, you have already seen what it took to silence the Pazzi, faking my death. And even yours seemed to be expected. The Yamnaya are an insufferable noise. And on the other hand, you’ve read too much, you know the answers to your questions. I’m not so naive as to think that you haven’t already read our future in those encyclopedias you’ve teleported from the future.

― Lor: -sighs approvingly- Go on.

― Giu: You know very well, brother, what I think, something as special as the Prima Sangoli should be stronger than the Danube, longer than the Estige and more moldable than the Zéfiro. However, I don’t understand why we give the Yamnaya so much power, we set up these theatres and hide from them, nor why the antediluvians are not content ruling their own time period and stop bothering with the rest. The condition of the necromancers is a rotten fruit from the basket that´s capable to rot the harvest.

― Lorenzo: Giuliano, that paradigm seems as difficult to answer as the face of our beloved “Dio” (god). The only thing that is as true as the succession of the moon to the sun, is that the power we Tinddaya hold, and our structural capacity as a society is not equivalent with our submission to the steppe people.

This ethic of ours, dilutes us in Purgatory and, in case of failing, it locks us in Dante’s hells. If we decided to dominate over humans openly, we would break the Masquerade and we would be very close to the necromantic idiosyncrasy, and I would rather be dead than resemble those barbarians -he sighs, kicking the floor-. I would love to have the gifts of Pericles and turn this republic into the new zenith of Europe.

― Giu: True, rebirth should last forever. However, brother, you know how the future unfolds. The generations to come are fortunate, they will find in our seed the inspiration for the new age. We will be a beam of light in the darkness, an age to be admired and longed for in the future.

― Lor: Be kind, dear Giuliano, and play that one I like so much, “Com’è triste Venezia”.

― Giu: -his fingers slide over the screen looking for the song- We live in a bubble, we are no more special than Alexander, Hadrian or Hannibal. So enjoy this fleeting moment. Lighten your spirit, brother. Read fiction, it enriches the mind and entertains the soul.

― Lor: Anyway… -the song plays and Lorenzo is left for a few seconds of pleasure-. Have they arrived yet?

Lorenzo takes a bell from the chest of drawers and rings it, a waiter knocks on the door and opens it.

― Lor: Have any of them arrived yet?

― Waiter: No, sir. But they should be coming soon.

― Lor: I despise those two, I bet mile fiorini that they are arguing -he says impatiently-. When they come to the palace, pressure them to come up quickly. They are marvellous separately, but insufferable together -he gestures with his hand, and the servant withdraws.

Through the arches of the Ponte Vecchio you can see the figure of two men on two contraptions resembling modern bicycles, made of wood, with ropes for the transmission of the pedals to the wheels, and both decorated with paper wings that move up and down. They move side by side, agitated by the rattle of the cobblestone floor, one overtaking the other, shouting and drawing the attention of all who passess by. As they advance, a group of people hurry to follow them, creating great interest in what they are discussing. Everyone knows about their boisterous arguments in the streets of Florence.

― Leonardo: Scusa, but as much as I think about it, it doesn’t make any sense, if you only work from the frontal plane, it wouldn’t be unreasonable to end up carving buttocks on David’s face (sorry, but as much as I think about it…).

― Michelangelo: Caro amico Leonardo (dear friend), I know well what you intend, piu per tutti (but for all) it is well known that you take for granted that my intellectual capacities are those of a stonecutter, but it is evident in my unequaled mastery, unattainable by your peasant and bastard perspective, that my mental concerns are of the highest level -he says, picking up a lump of coal in his pocket and throwing it at his interlocutor. Leonardo spins around, throwing him off balance and a few metres back-. You can’t even ride a mechanical donkey!

― Leo: Maledetto ragazzo! (damned lad!) Don’t make me fall if you don’t want this discussion to end with fists.

They leave the Piazza della Repubblica behind them, silhouetted against the dome of Santa Maria di Fiore. People shopping at the street stalls turn as they pass.

― Mich: È vero… (it’s true) Going back to the renaissance man, you know very well that all this is nothing more than theory and dignification of the profession, which undoubtedly needs your work, so you would do better to finish some work and stop imagining inventions, which although they are beautiful on paper, have no capacity for development at the present time. I hope you appreciate this reflection that if you don’t follow the theory of frontality, the one who will end up with an arse on his face will be you, as a talented man with the most unfinished projects.

― Leo: You are insopportabile (unbearable), Michelangelo. That mechanical donkey on which you enjoy speed is my invention. Get off it immediately if you are so displeased!

― Mich: Don’t be so defensive, I’m only joking… Some of your inventions are well worth your talent.

― Leo: I am a mathematician, physicist, inventor, visionary, artist, musician, religious, astrologer, archaeologist and theorist. My unfinished projects are the seed of the century to come. Do you speak of the modeling of light on the figure? Do you speak of the primal study on primordial work? Do you speak of multi-faciality and the kinetic spectator? -with each new sentence he raises his voice more and more. There is no one who does not look at them with full attention as they pass by-. Do you talk about the behaviour of colour, perspective and the human eye? How many things do you talk about to those who follow you and pick up your observations?… Of frontality, of the clay sketches that you say you don’t use, of your outbursts and little else.

If you are adored as a divine talent for something, it is because without knowing it, you are improving sculptures that do not seek to represent the skin, but the work that is born from within and explodes vibrantly, occupying the space.

That’s what you are and what you do without even knowing it, asino! (donkey!)

― Mich: Ascoltimi (listen to me), Leonardo, the steepingstone which the speeches of Rodin and Hildebrant revolve around, the man hated and admired by the entire 20th century, the sculptor studied by universities, the work to which citizens from all over the world make trek, it is my work! -he says, raising his hand to his chest with total solemnity-, my work, my legacy to humanity, which transgresses myself.

They both arrive at the palace. They cross the gate, a servant is waiting for them. They get off the mechanical donkeys and walk down the corridor.

― Waiter: Good morning, tutors. The prince is waiting for you in the study room with his brother. Please hurry, the master is impatient -the two artists nod while they continue to discuss as they walk down the corridor.

― Leo: Oh, come ingenuo! (how naive!) Your beloved disciple, Rodin practices multi- faciality and makes use of light as a sculptural element, Rodin uses the method of the exampeda, the same method that you so much despise, Rodin belongs to you as much as to me. I prefer not to speak of Hildebrant, his theorizing seems to me gratuitous and negative. As for the rest of the 20th century sculpture, it’s all yours, because it’s the only place in which you have a topic of conversation.

However, the one who will rack the brains of future generations with his inventions and hidden messages will be me -he says, touching his chest in mockery of Michelangelo’s oft-used gesture-. Never forget that.

― Mich: Porco pittore de second’ordine!!! (stinky second position painter) If you hadn’t been robbed from the Louvre you wouldn’t be half as famous as you are now. You may as well thank that vindictive little thief for stealing your dull Gherardini, but if it wasn´t for him you would be as famous as Crivelli, Colegiano or Tura.

― Leo: Rilassati, amico (relax, friend), maybe at some point they’ll dedicate a novel to you, or maybe a film… -he says with a cheerful look.

― Mich: Vechio inutile!!! (useless old man!) -he bites his lip, frowns and quickly raises his hand to strike when a voice interrupts.

― Lorenzo: Gentlemen, calm down! You know that violence is forbidden in my house, and even more between wise and talented distinguished people like yourselves. If you were to get along, your intelligence would help the world advance a century in just a decade.

Michelangelo, Leonardo, I have to thank you for answering to my call -they nod-, I beg you to leave this artistic show, the dome requires your talents with the most intense dedication and attention.

In the common room are Vives i March, Machiavelli, Caterina Sforza, Erasmus, Isabella D’Este, Lucrezia Borgia and her brother Cesare, Teresa of Cartagena, Dürer and Paracelsus. Go with them, they have been given all the information and are awaiting instructions.

It is the will of the dome that you be the chamberlains and lead the group of over 250 Prima Sangoli who heed our call throughout Europe. Know, however, that in the face of the steppe world, the European houses continue to struggle over power, yet the unity among the Prima Sangoli is strong in the face of antediluvian activity.

As you know, the antediluvians are able to use sand trenches to move objects through time, but we suspect that they have achieved the ability to move living things, an ability that was only exclusive to travellers until today. The ancients are capable of any unnatural mischief in order to gain power, they already control the shadows between time periods, and in this negligence of ours, for not monitoring their progress and not intervening in their actions, no more than six days ago a group of sorcerers was spotted walking the Via Appia Antica in Rome, near the mausoleum of the Imperatore Gallieno.

If they have achieved the ability to move the physical body through the plane of darkness and travel through time, it is our duty to stop such disgrace now, we cannot allow them to control history beyond their time.

― Leo: Allow me sir the audacity to contradict your words, but from the bottom of my heart, I strongly doubt that they have managed to use the plane of darkness to open intertemporal portals, the levels of fear in the world are not enough, they will have to wait for an overpopulated and overexploited planet to obtain enough energy. I am sure my respected Michelangelo confirms my words.

― Mich: If they have managed to open portals, I have not noticed any disturbance in the Teraghe`n. I fear, Sir, that it must be something different, but no less important or dangerous.

― Giuliano: In the west room, the Prima Sangoli are waiting for you, they have already arrived, they are working on it. You will be the ones supervising this campaign. Go and find out what’s going on, whatever it is, it’s not bonanza wind.

Leonardo and Michelangelo leave the room, walking in silence with a different expression on their faces.

― Leo: You are an alchimista di sangoli 1 -he whispers, staring at his partner-, I trust your talent wholeheartedly, but are you sure about what you said back there?

― Mich: -he stops, puts her hands to his face, rubs his eyes as he takes a deep breath, a couple of inhalations, opens his eyes and light shines on her iris- The past vibrates differently, it’s not them.

― Leo: Poi andiamo (well, let’s go) -he says quickening his pace.

Giuliano is leaning out of the window with a worried look on his face, Lorenzo writes instructions on a small roll of paper that he ties to the leg of a pigeon.

― Lorenzo: If they are right, I don’t want to think about who these sorcerers are. GIULIANO…?! -he exclaims, pouncing on his brother, who sways on the edge of the window, picks him up and gently drops him on the floor, knocks him down and lifts his legs. With a few sheets of paper he fans him. Giuliano is holding his head, looking pale and silent. Little by little he regains his colour and his breathing slows down. Lorenzo accompanies him to the chair, without saying anything, sits him down and waits beside him.

― Giuliano: …AAaa… Aaayy… -he mutters in pain-, the instructions must be changed, Leonardo and Michelangelo are right. The Teraghe`n has spoken, the Renaissance is not only the rebirth of the Prima Sangoli, it is also the rebirth of the necromancers.

― Lor: I beg your pardon? -he pauses with a skeptical expression.

― Giu: There is new necromancer blood, it doesn’t come from the past. Those magicians are our contemporaries -Lorenzo hands him a cup of water, he drinks it and drops his head against the backrest.

Lorenzo runs out into the corridor ringing the bell. The waiter appears.

― Waiter: Sir.

― Lor: Stay with my brother! He’s dizzy. I’m going to the West Room -he exclaims, running down the corridor-. Leonardo, Michelangelo! -he shouts as he enters the room, all the Prima sangoli stop anxiously, watching him-. The Teraghe`n has spoken to my brother, they are not antediluvians, they are a new generation of necromancers. Stop what you were doing, the goal is to track down these vandals across Europe, use your skills to track them down.

― Machiavelli: My lord, it is not possible, they disappeared more than two centuries ago from the face of the earth. My many travels around the world prove it.

― Lucrecia: They must have abandoned their hiding place to hunt. They must know something we don’t.

― Giu: Get those necromancers and whatever they’re looking for -they all nod. He leaves the room.

Lorenzo returns to the room where his brother is, touches his forehead, he has less fever and has regained his colour. He starts writing more notes to all the different houses in Europe that are part of the Dome and while Lorenzo writes, the waiter ties them to the pigeons.

Ω

An Asturian Mine That No Map Reaches

Valencia, at present.

Puuummm…! OOOhhh! Puuummm…! OOOhhh!

The same sound keeps repeating in the distance. The wind blows through the trees, it’s storming as the clouds gather over the building. The noise of the thunder steals the spotlight from the old Spital dels folls, orats e ignocents (hospital for the mad, the deranged and the innocent). All the lights are off, from the street the now municipal library looks the same as it always has in the early hours of the morning.

Puuummm…! OOOhhh! Puuummm…! OOOhhh!

The rain strikes the leaves of the dome windows, which are wide open. The shelves tremble in the light of the lightning. The water enters with every gust of wind, falling into the transept of the building, where the four arms of the Greek cross plan meet.

Inside, a dense light illuminates the centre of the room, but it’s not visible from the outside, it’s a reddish light and yet cold, hundreds of candles surrounding an empty central space. Puuummm…! OOOhhh! Puuummm…! OOOhhh! The chorus of voices of those present is interrupted only by the steady tapping of the cane against the marble of the floor, there are three figures dressed in feathered jet attire, they cover their faces with masks that resemble the beak of a raven. One of them, the one dressed in a cloak, carries the cane with which he hits the ground. The other two make arm movements, drawing figures in the air. Sitting on a chair, between the three of them, there is a young girl, naked, her face uncovered, her whole body covered in what looks like ideograms, there is no corner without those strange ash-black letters. She is tied to a very old chair, made of wood, full of cuts and scratches, with dark stains, it seems to be centuries old and to have had a gruesome use.

Puuummm…! OOOhhh! Puuummm…! OOOhhh! Outside the circle of candles, on the first floor and the balcony of the dome, there are more figures, standing motionless, staring into the centre of the room, the choral voices that break the silence after each stroke of the cane emanate from these figures. They hide their faces under a black cloak, with a huge hood from which only the end of a beak emerges. Only their hands are visible, palms up, the lineage of theTinddaya is drawn in, but the halo is dark, they are necromancers. A dark shadow emerges from their hands and falls to the ground, where it fluctuates, moving towards the three necromancers orchestrating the ritual.

Puuummm…! OOOhhh! Puuummm…! OOOhhh! The girl seems to be drugged, she does not react to anything that happens in the room, not even the storming rain manages to wake her up, the flowing darkness condenses under the girl, on the floor there are several objects that begin to dance as the shadows densify, there is a trepano, a rope knotted to a stone, some rings, a straitjacket and a ship’s rudder.

Poom…! Ooh! Poom…! Ooh! The rhythm of the cane quickens, the storm is right on top of the building. A horrible sensation flows through the building, screaming, wailing, whipping. The objects under the chair sway violently, the chair rattles, shaking the girl. Poom…! Ooh! Poom…! Ooh! The necromancer holding the cane places it on her body, the wood twists into a black snake that embraces her until it has covered her completely, it suffocates her causing her to spasm, between the rings salt water begins to gush out, the water is so heavy that it surges like a wave that breaks, extinguishing the candles, the three necromancers leave the circle. ZZRRRRUUUMMMMMMMM!!!! A bolt of lightning enters through the dome falling on the girl, the light blinds everything. The mist of shadows and the salt water burst into the air, all contained in a big bubble. The bookshelves full of books remain motionless, the water curls into a column that disappears beyond the dome. An abrupt silence. In the centre stands the young woman. All the necromancers gather around her.

The girl’s body is folded in on itself, a pale skin that seems to reflect all the light in the room, a fragile and hypnotic luminescence. She seems to want to wake up, her pulse has stopped, her face retains certain features, but she looks like another woman, her eyes are golden brown, with slit pupils. She gets up with difficulty, manages to stand up and reposition her body, takes a deep breath and stares at the three feathered necromancers around her, she pounces on one of them, without giving him time to react and she holds him by the neck, lifting him up. The necromancer holding the cane raises his hand, the darkness shines in his palm, the letters that cover the woman’s body begin to move, twisting her body, the woman opens her hand against her will. The necromancer falls to the ground, and the three repeat the gesture with their hands raised, the letters burn under the woman’s skin, she shrinks looking at them with anger, with an ancestral glow.

― Necromancer 1: You bear the Dup-Shimati written upon your body, Warmaster Kennin ji, son of Moor, the tablets of destiny stolen by Enki and now ours -she opens her cloak, stretching her arms out to their full length, there is an opaque darkness from which emerges the mask of Cräpatos. The woman stares at the effigy of the sentient Naga, her tense muscles relaxing as she realises she is trapped by the sorcerers-. Kennin ji, we are the ravens, Tinddaya of the necromancer lineage, we are your humble masters -she says, lifting her mask. It is Mercury who speaks. The others repeat the gesture.

― Radon: These are our faces, our voices, we implore you to remember them, as well as the smell of our blood with which it has been written upon the body you now occupy. Accept the offering we have made to you.

― Arsenic: We ask for your help to create a new order in this world, we must get rid of the antediluvians and the steppe people -the woman looks at him with an aseptic and alienated gesture.

― Mercury: A body in exchange for your help, your greatest virtue, to destroy. Do you consent? -he waits expectantly for the woman’s response, she nods-. Be it. Ongi etorri gure etxera, Kennin ji (welcome to our home).

― Radon: Be it -he says with a bow.

― Arsenic: Be it -he repeats the gesture.

Mercury opens a portal of darkness, walks through it, the other two ravens do the same, the woman walks behind them disappearing into the shadows. All the necromancers disappear from the library.

Ω

“Scary monsters, super creeps, Keep me running, running scared, Scary monsters…” That’s all you can hear, it’s almost midnight in the abandoned village of As Talladas. The lights are still on in one of the barns.

Pum, Pum, Pum! They bang on the door and come in.

― Nélida: Salma!, do I have to repeat to you that you don’t live alone? Tomorrow at eight o’clock we’ll all be on our feet -Salma dances around the room, approaches her sister and shakes her by the arms-. Salma!

― Salma: Dance with me, come on, we’re free… Can you believe it? Ha ha ha… I promise you that when the song is over I’ll turn off the music.

Nélida drops herself onto the bed, spreading her arms wide, taking up the mattress and singing the song at the top of her lungs. Salma continues to dance around the room. Roland appears and leans against the doorjamb, raises an eyebrow and smiles, Salma looks at him and with pleading hands smiles and continues singing. Roland approaches and sits down next to Nélida, they look at her as she sings, the music ends and Salma turns off the music player.

― Roland: Isn’t it a bit late for the festival?

― Sal: Yeah!, but not everyone has been locked up for 29 years. I do what I want, Roland -she says, shaking a shoulder to the beat-. There were two TV channels the last time I saw you in a good mood -she paces around the room with her hands in her pockets, striding, forcing a stern gesture-. I know music is annoying at this hour and at full volume, but I promise I don’t give a damn -she leaves her leg up in mid-stride, turns and walks out onto the overhang where there’s a futon on the floor, cushions and a couple of blankets, lies down and stares up at the sky-. I’m sorry, Roland, but I’m so happy that Sir David Bowie has to put a soundtrack to this moment. Turn off the light on your way out, good night -she says, covering herself and turning towards the forest.

In another house.

Beep! A message on Lázaro’s mobile phone.

― Nikoleta: Lázaro, go to sleep. Leave the console, it’s hard to sleep afterwards :* good night.

Beep! On Nikoleta’s mobile.

― Lázaro: Arreando xim pam pum (Doing it!).

The boy switches off the portable console and leaves it on the bedside table. He turns over on the mattress, covering himself with the sheets, and closes his eyes, which flutter under his eyelids.

― Láz: Nikoleta, are you awake?

― Nik: Talk to me on my mobile or come over here.

― Láz: You told me to go to sleep, so don’t ask me to come see you now.

― Nik: Don’t tease me, I’ve told you not to connect with me without my permission.

― Láz: I think the higher level mentalists are spying on us.

― Nik: They don’t, you and I don’t think about interesting things, and if they do, they do it without me knowing, so I don’t care.

― Láz: You’re so rude. I thought I’d say good night to you.

― Nik: Nighty Night, Lázaro, rest.

― Láz: Nikoleta…

― Nik: Tell me.

― Láz: Are you happy here? -It’s the same question every night.

― Nik: Yes, very much so. Shut up already.

― Láz: Yes. I’m also very happy here, what I like the most is to have bread from the oven for breakfast with Taramundi goat’s milk and tomato from the garden, puuufff….

― Nik: I’ll eat your breakfast tomorrow if you don’t get out of my head.

― Láz: You are lyring…

― Nik: Lázaro, good night.

― Láz: You are not lying… nanit (night) -his eyelids stop fluttering and he falls asleep.

Nikoleta is in her room, lying on the sofa, dressed in cat pyjamas, hood up to her eyebrows, one eye closed and the other open, looking at clothes on a stock fashion app. Her eyes close and she falls asleep curled up on the sofa.

Alejo arrives home sweating, wearing sports clothes, goes into the bathroom to take a shower, closes his eyes under the stream of boiling water that reddens his skin, taking a deep breath, POOMM!!!, a loud bang on the other side of the wall and he gets out of the shower in a hurry, opens the common door that leads to Jimena’s living room, and runs to the bedroom. There are little lights in the sockets illuminating the room. Jimena is on the floor, her arm reaching for the pillow, Alejo stops when he sees his sister on the floor, takes a few steps and reaches for the pillow.

― Jimena: Hmmm… -she puts it under her head without opening her eyes and continues sleeping.

― Alejo: Are you all right? -he says bending down, bringing his face close to his sister’s.

― Jim: You sleep well here.

― Ale: I know… -he stands still in front of her, not knowing what to say, arms balled up in fists.

― Jim: Go away, brother. I’m good.

― Ale: Take the pill -he takes her hand and puts it in her palm.

― Jim: No, they slow me down, and I’m already slow as it is -she turns and looks at her brother, half-smiling. Alejo, without losing his pose, pushes her with his foot.

― Jim: What are you doing?

― Ale: Get up, you’re not a dog.

― Jim: Salma sleeps like this as well.

― Ale: -he raises an eyebrow- Well, what a pair of bitches -he offers her his hand, she looks at it for a moment and turns away. Alejo bends down and lifts her up, she offers no resistance.

― Jim: Ggrrrrrrrrr -grunts.

― Ale: You have blood on your lip.

― Jim: It’s nothing -she says licking her lip.

― Ale: You have to rest, take your pills, dammit.

― Jim: -she stares at him- Tomorrow.

― Ale: Yeah… I’m going to sleep, do you need anything? -he says in the doorway.

Jimena hesitates, not daring to speak her mind. Alejo looks at his sister, who lowers her head, avoiding his gaze. Thirty years in a prison of darkness, the man imagines for a moment and a shiver runs through his body. He crosses the room and opens the windows wide, the same with the door, leaving the hall light on. He gets into bed with Jimena.

― Jim: What are you doing?

― Ale: Sleep, and you should do the same.

Jimena plops down next to her brother, looks at him in detail, there was a time when she couldn’t remember what his face looked like or what his voice sounded like. She closes her eyes and finally sleeps. That night she has no nightmares.

Outside the area of houses, in a meadow by the river bank, you can spot a bluish glow shining, Koiki meditates with his eyes closed, breathing deeply. Next to him, there are some disciples, they are sitting on the ground, but their light is not so intense.

A little further down, where the trees surround the dwellings, in the largest oak tree on the northern slope, from where you can see the river and hear its murmur, there is a house built on its top, with different heights respecting the peculiarity of its branches, there is a bridge that connects with the ground. There is no glass in the windows, there are some walls and some roof, just under which Cornelia sleeps in a woollen sack with three cats and two dogs sleeping huddled next to her.

Ω

It is 8:00 in the morning. The houses in the village are empty. A few hundred metres away there is an old abandoned mine that has been converted into a work and learning space. Everyone is there. The building is made of sheet metal, beams and a cyclopean wall, and despite the simplicity of its lines and its avant-garde character, it blends in with the mountain without clashing with it. It is full of oculi that bring light into the rooms.

As Talladas is an abandoned village, the houses are preserved as they were found, but they have been given a new look to make them habitable. They have been restored and new spaces have been built, all integrated into nature.

A team of 11 Yamnaya clean and repair the houses, tend the vegetable garden and the animals. About 20 minutes from As Talladas there is a small village of people who live in the countryside where the crisis has left the few families who live there without financial resources. They travel in 3 vans that run without diesel and with which they come and go every day.

In the gardens there is another group, in the kitchens they are collecting breakfast and preparing meals. They don’t know what the Tinddaya are, why there are so many people in the classrooms and workshops if they never see anyone arrive, if there are no cars to bring them, nor do they know what they do, but they don’t ask questions. They are nice people and they pay well, with their arrival they have brought a dignified life back to the village.

The workshop classrooms are quite peculiar, there is no single teacher, there are several high-level Tinddaya in the same classroom, attending individually and in groups. The apprentices are of different ages, they are divided according to the level of Teraghe`n and knowledge. During the morning, in the first hours, they are taught according to their lineage, and then grouped according to their interests. In many cases, Tinddaya often achieve similar results from different lineages.

Each of the groups bring together nationalities from all over the world, and they communicate with each other through intercoms in their ears, translating the language of the speaker in real time. However, some of the Tinddaya speak in a language of their own, an ancient language, vernacular to the species, and although some of it is forgotten, it is intertwined with English, Spanish and Chinese.

The language is taught by Giudo, a Venetian, son of a merchant lineage, he is one of the few Tinddaya whose family language has been the vernacular since the 10th century, he speaks 15 languages fluently and knows a bit of six more, he is also fluent in sign language. His ability as an architect is that of constructing language, but what is really remarkable is that he is able to speak the language of the person he is interacting with while addressing them, once they stop speaking and he loses focus on them, he loses the ability to speak that language.

In the mixed workshops, different lineages come together according to the abilities of each individual to generate synergies, enhance the abilities of the other lineages, improving the development of the Tinddaya. In the knowledge workshops, the sensitives show the Tinddaya how to feel the Teraghe`n and be part of it. In the history workshops, each lineage and its history is studied.

Lago, Ailén and Eir are sitting on a bench watching Lázaro carefully, they have a tablet on which they can see the parameters and image in real time of what the boy is seeing. Lago is the doctor who has implanted the bionic arm in the young man, Eir is the architect who has developed the material that joins the biological nerves with the robotic limb, Ailén is the engineer who develops the mutable drones. On the grassy field there is a circuit with several posts and obstacles for drone flight, Lázaro is standing in the centre of the space, wearing a helmet with binoculars and headphones, a series of attachments on the bionic arm. He is immersively piloting the drone, which moves at full speed in obedience to the movements of his hand and arm.

― Lázaro: Have you noticed that? When I do the hard trick 360º and go through the 180º tunnel from 120 to 200 km/h I can’t control it to the right, but to the left I don’t feel the same.

― Ailén: Bring the ship here and I’ll take a look at it.

― Eir: No, wait, keep flying, go back to 360º.

― Láz: Okay -Lago stands up and stands behind Lázaro watching his arm movement. He does 360º.

― Lago: OK, that’s it. Land it, please -the boy pilots the craft to the ground and removes his goggles.

― Eir: What did you see?

― Lag: It’s not the ship, it’s well calibrated. It’s Lázaro’s arm, it’s not articulated to make that movement to the left. The bionic arm is adapted to make human movements. I can make it do 360º turns in both directions, but you’ll have to learn to control it again and it won’t look as natural, but it will be much more accurate than if I built you a controller for your left arm.

― Eir: The substance is specialised on muscle’s sensory nerves. I could raise the substance to a mother state, so that it covers the spinal cord and reaches the thalamus, specialising on these structures. With a higher level of connection the physical manipulation will still be necessary, but your mind will be extremely sensitive and the piloting very precise. It can be interesting in conjunction with Lago’s proposal.

― Láz: I don’t care what it looks like, I like the idea, I can’t wait to try it.

― Ail: Your attention field is now capable of moving 45 000 U and altering the shape of the ship with an attention of 41.000/50.000. If everything goes as I hope and you continue your training, you will soon be one with this ship model. However, I have almost finished the 100.000 U prototype, I would like to be able to test it, so you will have to increase your flight hours to be able to fly it.

― Láz: It’s almost double! -he hesitates for a moment-. But if the arm gives me more freedom, I’m sure I’ll be able to control the 100,000 U.

― Eir: I’m glad to hear that, I don’t think it will take long to get the sensory driver ready. To which we add intervention and adaptation time.

― Lag: Your rearticulated arm will be ready in a couple of days.

― Ail: Even if we have it ready, there is no hurry, Lázaro.

― Eir: Adaptation cannot be accelerated, it could lead to injury. Let’s go in order, it is important.

― Láz: Molt bé (very well), without rushing it’s better, training leaves my brain flat.

― Roland: -walks up to them- How’s it going? I was looking forward to watching you flying the drone but it looks like I’m late.

― Láz: No way, we’re talking about upgrading my bionic arm and increasing the drone’s units. There are a few things that the current arm doesn’t allow me to do. Watch -Lázaro puts on his goggles and starts flying it. The four of them look at the boy.

― Rol: How does it evolve?

― Ail: Lázaro is a prodigy at controlling external artificial intelligence, connecting with the machine as if it was part of him. He now moves about 41.000 U, but we suspect that with the speed at which he learns and with his level of Teraghe`n, he will soon be moving five-zero ships -Eir and Lago nod.

― Rol: What about the sensory conductors, could we fuse them with Salma’s tissues to provide cover for the fighters?

― Lag: I am working on semi-rigid defences, which would cushion the blow and return the armour to its original state. Even detecting the hit moments before and condensing the defence by making it harder to take the impact. But this does not depend on the fighter, but on a very powerful AI.

― Eir: As the substance stands now, without opening a conduit to the spinal cord, I can’t work at this level. It all depends on whether there are fighters willing to go digital. However, I could do something at the cutaneous level, it would serve to accelerate and enhance the body-suit connection.

― Lag: And your project, Roland?

― Rol: The truth is that I spend more time with students and their projects than with contact lenses. I still haven’t adjusted the magnification and vision changes, but if I manage to finish it, it will be the end of glasses and telescopes. Yesterday I was looking at the moon in high definition as if it was a watermelon in my hand, but it’s very dizzy, I have to improve the stabilizers.

Ω

Salma and Nikoleta walk through Cartagena’s streets on a sunny morning, full of tourists. Salma is holding a device in her hand, in the shape of a pocket watch, the dial is a green reticulated screen with a white dot flashing and the numerical metres oscillate. Both are walking along gazing at people while they chat animatedly.

― Nikoleta: What is it like to be away again after so many years?

― Salma: Strange and familiar, I can’t get used to so many people, so many cars, so much of everything -Salma focuses her gaze on a distant point of the port-. Actually, I’m the one who’s different -music plays-. I’ll always like it here -they walk to the promenade, a small concert is taking place on a stage, there are a lot of people ravended together enjoying themselves and dancing.

― Nik: What was the last concert you attended?

― Sal: I could never forget it, we went to Zaragoza to see Héroes del Silencio on the 9th of October in the year 1991. It was brutal.

“Pi Pi Pi!” the phone rings interrupting them, Elizabeth takes out her mobile and switches it on, downloading the group photo “Ha ha ha ha ha ha” she laughs in amusement.

― Sal: Let me see -she looks and only half-smiles-. It’s really bad. All day long with the memes.

― Nik: You don’t get it because you’re not a millennial. Look at it again and tell me it’s not funny -it’s a picture of Julio Iglesias saying: You’re Tinddaya and you know it.

― Sal: OK, it’s kind of funny, but in general they’re very bad.

“Pi Pi Pi!”, Roland’s message: “please avoid this in the group, you have a group exclusively for memes”.

― Nik: Wow, I can’t imagine living with him for 24 hours.

― Sal: -arching her eyebrows- He has his things, but he’s exceptional.

“Pi Pi Pi!”, message from Roland: “…well, I kind of approve this meme because it’s so good XD”.

Nikoleta and Salma laugh as they read it and continue walking through the harbour in the opposite direction to the university.

Puup …. Puup… Pup… Pu.! Salma’s contraption beeps, the two become alert and watch.

― Sal: She’s less than 50 m away -there’s no one near her except the people on the terrace of a high-rise restaurant-. Do you have her location?

― Nik: I can’t quite see it, it’s like there’s something weird, like the essence is in two places at once… -she says looking up, but only clients sitting by the railing can be seen-. It has to be there. We don’t have anyone walking that close.

― Sal: We’re 20 metres north. It’s there, for sure -she stops suddenly in front of the menu-. Look! they have marinara, I’ll buy you some.

They both enter the building, on the top floor is the terrace, there are people eating. It is clear to Nikoleta who they are. There are two women sitting at a table away from the rest. They have very similar features, they are very stout and look tall, chatting animatedly as they eat.

Salma asks for a table next to them and the waiter prepares the table for them.

― Waiter: What will the ladies have?

― Sal: Please, two beers and two marineras… well, you’d better bring three -the waiter leaves and Salma turns her attention to Nikoleta-. Which one is it?

― Nik: Dunno. When I see the target, I hear it clearly. But now it’s different, I hear the sound with echo and as if it were two frequencies.

― Sal: I can’t adjust the detector any further, it’s up to you -she says, fiddling with the device.

― Nik: I’ll call them -Salma nods. She picks up the phone and texts the Trisib, they answer telepathically.

― Trisib 1: -sounds in Nikoleta’s head- Concentrate on the sound so we can hear it -Nikoleta holds her breath-. They’re next to you, they’re two fighters, not one.

― Nik: -answers with her thought- I see them, it’s the two women.

― Trisib 2: We hear the essences, not the sex of the individual.

― Nik: …OK -looks at Salma with a weird gesture.

― Sal: So…

― Nik: Well, it looks like they’re both Tinddaya -Salma smiles with a mouthful of food. Nikoleta takes a sip of beer and empties the bottle-. I’ll use it as a conversation starter -she winks at Salma and turns around-. Hi, I’ll buy you a round -she smiles broadly, unaware that she has a piece of red pepper between her teeth.

― Woman 1: No, thank you -she gives her a half-smile and they both go back to chatting. Nikoleta remains silent in a strange silence, seeming to have no influence on them. Salma hides her face in her hands, mitigating a giggle. She soon pulls herself together.

― Sal: Everything all right? -Nikoleta shrugs, shakes her head and turns around again.

― Nik: Hey!, we like the same thing, we’re Mate Palate -she says pointing to their plates with leftovers, the women look at her with some discomfort-. Can you recommend something else?

― Woman 2: Anything is fine -she says, and they go back to their business.

― Nik: Holly Dolly -she whispers, looking at Salma. It isn’t working. I don’t get it, I’m irresistible -she rolls her eyes and opens her arms.

― Trisib 3: They are type 1 gladiators, they are overriding you without being aware of it. Don’t close the channel, concentrate, put your hands on their hands and squeeze hard.

― Nik: They’re huge, they scare me.

― Trisib 2: We are going to project, the moment you touch them, we make them immobile. Do it.

Salma looks at her expectantly, she knows she is talking to the Trisib, when she talks to them her lips move subtly and her eyes vibrate. Nikoleta gets up, stands next to the table, looks at them with a smile, the others return her an impatient look, the girl swallows saliva and places her hands on the women’s hands. They react very quickly and the gesture turns violently, but the mere touch of the girl paralyses them, the Trisib project images and memories on them. Their gaze is lost and they suffer small tremors, they start to babble.

― Trisib 1: That’s it, Nikoleta, let them go.

Nikoleta releases them, Salma jumps off the table and pulls Nikoleta away from the women. The two women get up violently and move into an attack position, staring at the girl with bloodshot eyes.

― Woman 1: What the fuck have you done to us?! -the rest of the diners look at them. The waiter approaches the table cautiously.

― Sal: Sorry. We didn’t mean to scare you -she says, stepping back-. We are like you, we also have “special qualities” -the waiter approaches.

― Waiter: Is everything all right, ladies Howard? -he asks, looking at the combatants and the other two women alternately.

― Woman 2: No, it’s not OK -she replies nervously.

― Sal: We are leaving. No problem. We’ll leave you this mobile phone -she hands it to the waiter who takes it warily-. There’s only one number in the phonebook -she takes out a 20 euro note and hands it to the waiter. They both walk hurriedly towards the exit.

Back on the harbour promenade they catch their breath. Nikoleta is still frightened after the sisters’ reaction. Standing with a twisted gesture they are really impressive.

― Sal: Did they tell you to do that?

― Nik: Yes.

― Sal: You can definitely do this even without your skills -she says, holding her by the shoulders and staring at her-. Follow your instincts as you have done so far, Trisib have no social skills, don’t always do what they ask you to -she winks at her. Nikoleta nods with a smirky smile.

More than half an hour later Salma’s phone rings. Salma smiles at Nikoleta.

― Sal: It’s them.

― Nik: You speak -she says with a hand gesture, disengaging herself from the call.

― Woman 1: Where do we find you?

― Sal: We are on top of the wall, at the first viewpoint after climbing the spiral staircase. We are waiting for you here -they hang up.

Ω

“Knock, knock, knock”

― Nélida: Come in -the door opens and Ekaitz enters. Inside the room, Cornelia and Nélida are drinking coffee while they chat, standing in front of some notebooks, collating numbers-. Hello, Ekaitz.

― Cornelia: Hi. Mmm… you look surprised, anything new?

― Ekaitz: Surprise! Yes, but not the good kind. I heard about this when I was studying medicine, but I never wanted to give it the slightest veracity. These last few days I suspected that this would be the result, but I can’t get over my astonishment, it’s incredible.

― Nél: You really didn’t expect this? After what you know, are you surprised by something like this coming from the Yamnaya?

― Ekaitz: I’ve been Tinddaya for six months, of course I’m surprised. I became a doctor to cure diseases, to save lives.

― Cor: You were already a doctor before you qualified, you have always been a doctor.

― Ekaitz: But this is creepy -he says, waving his arms-, the three variants we have sequenced are man-made.

― Nél: It won’t be the first thing you discover, don’t get discouraged and concentrate on finding the cures.

― Ekaitz: I have some in phase 3, it’s easy with our technologies. But it seems macabre to me. I can’t understand it.

― Cor: We’re all descended from the Naga, it’s in our genes to be bad -she sips her coffee sparingly.

― Nél: You’d rather drink that coffee with a goat than with me -she says, smiling. Cornelia shrugs and takes another sip-. Tell us exactly what you’ve found.

― Ekaitz: We have managed to make a nano robot capable of destroying the disease in an adult in less than 24 hours, but for that we would have to introduce the robot into its body. So we have varied the behaviour pattern. We release the robot into a reservoir of drinking water, the robot when it is swallowed stays in the body and never leaves it, only one robot per person stays in the body. It allows us to cover a lot of the population.

― Nél: Bravo! -he claps his hands on his chest-. No doubt this is why you were on Mérida’s list.

― Cor: -slowly puts the cup on the table- Anything else?

― Ekaitz: Division 2 is working with cancer, we have reduced an E4 to an E2. Division 3 is working on Alzheimer’s. But the most interesting thing is the advances with the butterfly skin of Division 4. Although we are a bit stuck with some procedures.

― Nél: I am sorry that there is none of your lineage with experience left for you to advance. Lineages oscillate in each era. It’s not a concern, except for the alchemists, of which there are only two left.

― Ekaitz: This is something Genevieve is working on, what is the factor that decides which lineage is inherited, but she is not able to sequence it because it is closely linked to Teraghe`n, which could help us to recover lineages such as the alchemist.

― Nél: The remaining alchemists are not human, they are mythological beings born out of magic. With them the lineage will end.

― Eka: Do you know them? -and what are they like?

― Cor: Specials.

― Eka: I mean physically, what’s so mythological about them?

― Nél: They are half man, half wolf.

― Ekaitz: Wow… do you think there is any chance of seeing them in person? It would be extraordinary to be able to analyse their structure.

― Cor: I don’t think so -she says finishing her cup of coffee.

― Nél: It will be hard to convince them, but we’ll try -she says with a half-smile.

― Eka: Thank you -he leaves slightly satisfied after sharing his concern with the women.

Cornelia goes back to her notebook, she is keeping track of the expenses incurred by the inhabitants of As Talladas in the Yamnaya society.

― Nél: What’s that number over there, it doesn’t add up.

― Cor: It is a card payment.

― Nél: But with the common goods card?

― Cor: The other one was wrong… -she says resignedly.

― Nél: Ha ha ha ha -laughing-, it’s all right, silly, what I want to know is what you bought.

― Cor: A charm for the bracelet… -she mumbles.

― Nél: Ha, ha, ha… -still laughing.

― Cor: I knew you would laugh -she says with a serious smile.

― Nél: I thought you disowned those things.

― Cor: I don’t deny anything. I think it’s very funny, it’s a cat charm -she lifts up her sleeve and shows her a hemp bracelet with a silver kitten on it-. There are many more and very pretty ones… -she bursts out laughing. They both laugh.

Ω

― Professor: I have asked Mrs Tesla to come and assess the five projects we have here today. However, Andrea is with her team testing the artificial chuva (rain) in the Sahara desert, so we have a live broadcast via a minimee -a small robot loads the holographic image of the woman, which is fully represented on its own structure, copying her movements.

― Tesla: Zdravo (hello), guys, girls, nice to see you. Aecio told me that you have made great progress and are excited about your proyekatas (projects). Go for it.

A girl with hair stiffened by static electricity steps forward.

― Girl: -when she separates her lips to speak there is a click- Our project is the electrodrome, we have been inspired by your grandfather’s work to transport electrical energy through the earth without the use of cables. These spheres are distributed along the street as if they were street lamps, they are capable of absorbing all the energy flow generated in their environment, people coming and going, the sun, the air, passing cars, anything that gives off energy, is absorbed, and then returns it to the electric current and can power any electrical device.

― Andrea: Dobro! (bravo!) -think about the real possibilities of the invention-. My iaio (grandpa) would be thrilled… BROOOMM!!!! -a roar is heard interfering with the minimee-. We’ve got it, Aetius! Did you hear it? It was a thunder. We have a cloud of water above us -she exclaims excitedly-. See you tonight, thank you all.

The connection is shut and the minimee is left back with its droid figure, everyone continues to stare at it with excitement in their eyes.

― Aecio: You have done a wonderful job. Tesla was impressed.

The Lady Corsairs of la Playa de Cartagena

The two women are walking towards the wall, they must be over 185, wearing short-sleeved shirts and some scars. Both are wearing aviator glasses and a bracelet with the American and Japanese flags.

― Joanna: I still get goosebumps, fuck. Have you seen what I’ve seen?

― Nakano: I’ve seen those people… -she scratches her head hard, trying to get rid of the images.

― Joa: What if we have been drugged? Middle Eastern, Eastern European agents?

― Nak: Those two are from some neighbourhood around here. Besides, they’re two wimps, they couldn’t do anything to us -he says confidently-. They were scared when we marked them.

― Joa: Yes, I’ve seen it. But they annoy me and we are going voluntarily to meet them.

― Nak: It’s easy, if they do anything weird, they go outside -she says, touching something stiff on his back under her T-shirt.

― Joa: -nods- Done. But have you thought about what to do after what they’re going to tell us?

― Nak: Whatever it is, I’ll need a good explanation.

― Joa: So many weird people, fuck.

Both women arrive on the terrace, Nikoleta and Salma get up.

― Nikoleta: Hey!, sorry for the scare earlier -she says restrained. They look at her for a moment.

― Nak: Nothing, it was mutual.

― Salma: Not entirely mutual… -she gestures with her hands comparing their heights. The others shrug their shoulders-. Anyway, do you want us to tell you who you are, or shall we take you straight there?

― Joanna: No, and better not to touch us. Tell us what this is about.

― Sal: You can see it in situ, no other flash is necessary.

― Nakano: Where?

― Sal: Any door will do. It’s across the street, in the doorway of that building.

― Joa: I beg your pardon?

― Nik: It doesn’t give you a headache or anything weird. We move like this all over the world.

The four of them arrive at the entrance to the building, a building that still has its facade from the XIX with a new glass structure at the top. Salma puts her hand in her pocket, “nexokey” she says, places it on the lock of the door, turns the key and on the other side a new space appears, a large hall that has nothing to do with what can be seen through the glass of the doorway. Joanna and Nakano look around, wary. People are crossing the room. Footsteps approach.

― Roland: Hello, good afternoon, my name is Roland -he greets them with a smile-. Come in, please -the women are suspicious. Roland takes his mobile phone out of his pocket, activates the GPS, marks the position in Asturias, shows the location to the women, who exchange a surprised look.

― Sal: Don’t think about it, we’re more afraid of you -he says with a grimace and a low voice.

― Nak: OK -she looks at her sister who nods.

The four of them walk through the door, Salma removes the key and closes the door. Cartagena is already far away.

― Rol: I know it’s a hard pill to swallow -he says as he walks down the corridor-. We’re not waking up adult people, unless the Teraghe`n is very high and the skills are obvious.

― Nak: Do you know who we are?

― Rol: No, we only knew of your existence from your Teraghe`n.

― Joa: And what is our obvious skill for you?

― Rol: …You are first-rate fighters, you have blocked Nikoleta. But let me introduce you to the rest.

― Joa: Ok -she says seriously.

Roland knocks on the door and opens it. Jimena, Alejo, Koiki, Nélida, Cornelia, Lago and the Trisib are there. They are all seated except for the Trisib who are standing at the windows.

― Koiki: Joanna and Nakano. I would like to welcome you. There are many more of us here and soon you will meet all the Tinddaya.

Lago approaches with a small device in his hand.

― Lago: It’s a Teraghe`n test, a prick on the fingertip and we’ll know the result -the twins offer him their hand, Lago proceeds, they stare without quite understanding what’s going on-. It oscillates between level one and zero load. It is curious.

Jimena glances at the women from time to time, but continues to stare out of the window, lost in thought. One of the Trisib watches her. Jimena gets up without saying anything and leaves the room. Alejo stares at where his sister was a moment ago.

― Trisib 1: It was hard for us to get in, they are very powerful and they did it innately -they look uncomfortable.

― Cornelia: Do you want to tell us something about yourselves?

― Nak: What specifically do you want to know?

― Cor: What do you do, for example?

― Nak: We work for the US army, we are generals in the navy. We have been in Cartagena for a year now, with a collaboration programme with Tercio de Armada. After the last combat mission in the Middle East, we decided to take a year away from combat zones, and now we command strategies from the Cartagena base.

― Joa: On mission, our job is to strategize how to annihilate extremist groups. We lead special forces teams, silent and invisible in war. We are specialised in hand-to-hand combat and the use of bladed weapons.

― Trisib 2: That’s a euphemism for elite assassins -he says with an aseptic gesture.

― Ale: Azazel, shut up -the Trisib nods indifferently.

― Lago: I would like to know if you have felt anything that would have made you suspect your condition?

― Nak: It happens only with Chi-kung. Depending on the movement, and it doesn’t always happen, our skin lights up with different colours and shapes.

― Joa: At first we thought we had been infected by a biological weapon.

― Lag: It is clear that there are skills that are active and others that need to be developed. Would it be possible to see any of those movements that cause luminescence? -the women nod.

Lago says “Nox” (night) and the lights go out softly, they both get into position and perform the steps, under the clothes the white light appears and the lines expand on their hands and face until it illuminate their eyes, everyone observes the movements they make, until they finish and their skin returns to its natural state. The lights in the room are switched on again.

― Koi: Each figure and colour is an action of attack or defence towards another lineage. It is very interesting to see this for the first time and imagine how far you can go with the right knowledge and training.

― Nak: That sounds good.

― Joa: Does learning from you mean staying here?

― Rol: No need actually. We will provide a bridging door for each of you. You will open a door from your house that will lead directly to the entrance hall of the Mine. Feel free to come and go as you please -the woman nod.

― Joa: That’s fine -she looks at her sister-. Shall we call it a day and go home?

― Nak: Ok.

― Salma: Yes, of course. I’ll walk with you again -both women follow Salma into the hall. She opens the door and they appear in a small alley next to the Roman forum. The door is painted on the wall-. I can’t take you any closer.

― Joa: It’s OK, it’s not that far from the base.

― Sal: Tomorrow at 8 o’clock there will be someone on the other side of the door, knock 3 times on the surface and they will open it for you. If you want to stay, we will install the bridging doors.

― Nak: -they both nod- See you tomorrow.

― Sal: See you tomorrow -closes the door.

Back in the meeting room. Spark slips in among those present who are commenting on the women’s talent. Deer comes running in behind.

Everyone leaves the room. Salma runs after Spark, with Deer a few steps behind.
