― Spa: Already?

― Nél: Tomorrow at 12 o’clock they will be waiting for you at their front door. You have the address in your profile. Thanks for being there. See you tomorrow. Good night.

― Spa: Oidhche mhath (goodnight).

It’s 12 noon the next morning. Spark and Deer have been waiting at 15 Juan Manuel Robles’ Street for some time now. The door opens, the woman appears with three suitcases and the girl is dragging some bags. Deer runs over and helps them.

― Deer: Madainn mhath! (good morning!) You don’t need to move them any more, our transport is right there at the door.

― Carmen: Good morning. I don’t understand what you mean.

― Deer: Don’t you worry, you’ll get the way of it. You’ll be surprised, I’m sure, but as Nélida and Roland told you, our centre has the most advanced technology there is -he says, pulling the knob from his shoulder strap. Spark is already beside them carrying the women’s gear. Deer places the knob and the door opens into the foyer of the Mine, Nélida and Roland are waiting for them-. Et voilà (and this is it!) Here we are.

― Carmen: What? -she says with a discomposed expression, the girl smiles from ear to ear.

― Lux: Wow… That’s so cool! -she says, pulling her mother to the other side. The door closes.

― Roland: Welcome to your new home, this is the foyer of the Mine. A unique centre in the world.

― Nél: Carmen, you’re going to love it, doors like this are some of the most basic gadgets we develop here. You’ll soon get used to all the adventures your daughter will tell you about what she’s going to study here. If you want, leave the children’s bags and come with us -the woman nods, still amazed. The girl is very willing.

Nélida and Roland accompany them through the common area, and show them the starter classrooms where they can see small gadgets that are easy to explain. The woman, as she sees other children and adults, relaxes. Lux keeps her composure but is wide-eyed. After the little tour, they go outside the Mine.

― Rol: This is the outside of the Mine, As Talladas. A few of us live here. The rest of us use the bridge gates to get here from all over the world. Carmen, you are going to live here and you are our guests. However, if you prefer to work, there are people from the next village who come every day to work here, you can join them and even go to the village. Again, I insist, this is a very little known organisation that is dedicated to capturing great skills, one of them, your daughter, to be here, it is necessary that you keep silent about what you see. That’s all we ask.

― Carmen: Yes… -the woman nods without being able to articulate any more words.

― Nélida: Lux will have access to student areas, however, those who are family members have certain restrictions. It’s nothing secret, but you could access areas where there are very sensitive developments and we prefer to avoid that from happening. Regarding the housing and outdoor common areas, they are for everyone. And down there you have the granaries, you already have your own. You can go down there, you’ve already got your bags there, and you can start settling in. It’s 12:45, at 14:00 meals are served in the dining room, and after lunch, Lux, you come with us, we’ll start the instruction. Do you agree?

― Lux: Yep.

Both walk towards their new home with a face that combines disbelief and happiness.

It is 15:30 in the afternoon. “KNOCK, KNOCK, KNOCK” Koiki and Roland wait at the new arrival’s door. The little girl opens the door, while the mother stays behind.

― Roland: Hi, Carmen. Hello, Lux. This is Koiki -the sensitive bows his head in greeting-, he’ll be your tutor. We understand that your talent, although still to develop, deserves special attention. I leave you with him.

― Koiki: Nice to meet you, Lux -he offers her his hand, she takes it-. We’re going to cross the river to the valley area, today I’ll tell you about us so you know everything there is here.

― Lux: Okay. See you later mum -the mother smiles looking down the hill at them. She closes the door and begins a tour around the common facilities.

― Koiki: It is complex to understand in its full form, there are many details, but I will try to be very concrete and simple -the girl nods-. All of us that you will see here are Tinddaya, a different culture to humans, although in appearance we are very similar. Each one of us, just like you, has special abilities that can be developed, but cannot evolve very much, because we have a level of energy that conditions us. That energy is called Teraghe-n-Magheq, there are people in whom it is very high, and they have great abilities, and people with a low level, that have less abilities. You have a high level. The skills depend on which Tinddaya group you belong to, each group has different skills. It’s like Pokémon, there are fire Pokémon, water Pokémon, plant Pokémon… -the girl nods.

We, as a species, are descendants of a culture that comes from another part of the universe, the Naga. The Naga created humans for labour and the Tinddaya so that the Naga species could continue to live on this planet, which is why we look so much like humans, but we are different.

Am I going too fast?

― Lux: No. I want to know more, tell me about the Naga, please.

― Koi: We have 8, Alaris father of the architects, Gala Évora mother of the physicians, Dhyavod father of the engineers, Ràdamus of the alchemists, Cräpatos mother of the travellers, Shamkra Baem mother of the fighters, Niorai mother of the mentalists and Moor father of the Necromancers, and all together they are the parents of the ninth lineage, the sensitive ones.

Among the Naga, as in every family, there are good and bad relationships, but the one who had the most differences with the rest was Moor. To take care of everything the Naga had built, and especially to take care of Moor, 7 guardians were created, one for each Naga.

Shall I go on?

― Lux: Yes.

― Koi: Moor, angry, gave birth to another 7 guardians, but in his dark version.

And with them we end our most distant origins.

As you can imagine, all this always ends up in fights, not only among the Naga themselves, but also among the humans themselves, among the Tinddaya themselves, and of course between humans and Tinddaya. Right now, this thing you see, has been built to avoid conflict with the humans, we hide. We are the forgotten ones to them, the oblivium.

― Lux: I get it, the smarter guys at school hide from each other.

― Koi: Right. To protect yourself, sometimes it’s better not to be seen. There are only a few ravens around, but we’ll soon come to an agreement with them.

― Lux: What are ravens?

― Koi: They are Tinddaya belonging to the lineage of necromancers.

― Lux: And what lineage do I belong to?

― Koi: You are a lunar alchemist, from the lineage of alchemists -the girl looks at him waiting for more details-. And besides being Tinddaya, you are the daughter of a human, you are between two worlds.

― Lux: Can I tell who Tinddaya are just by looking at them?

― Koi: We all carry a mark on our hand that rises when we utter the appropriate words “Ahul Achi Naga Tinddaya” (greetings Tinddaya, son of the Naga) -and the symbol on his hand glows brightly, causing the girl’s hand to glow as well.

― Lux: Wow, it’s so cool! do it again, please -Koiki does it and the girl repeats it, her hand lights up-. THIS IS AMAZING!!!! -she exclaims, running around the man. When the glow disappears, she looks around for another Tinddaya. That one over there… mmmm -she says, looking at a young man sitting on a stone bench writing in a notebook-. I think that boy is. I don’t know.

― Koi: That boy over there is a sensitive, he has a peculiar sensitivity for observation. It is difficult to know the lineage of our peers without awakening the Teraghe`n, but you will be able to know when you learn to listen to the universal energy. That’s why Spark knew about you when he touched your hand -the girl looks at him with narrowed eyes, understanding the connection between everything that has happened in the last few hours.

Ω

The voice of the Trisib sounds in unison in the heads of some of the Tinddaya in As Talladas. They all act without startle so as not to frighten the rest.

― Trisib: It is manifesting itself -the sensation has come as a surprise and it has caught them in the middle of their meditation, breaking their peace-. It fluctuates, but it is here, it is waiting for permission to appear.

― Alejo: No permission.

― Salma: Are you OK? Your voice is shaking -no one is using the intercom in the first answers, they talk directly to them.

― Roland: Intercoms activated. Joanna, Nakano, Hajnalka coming in. To the hall, please -the sisters answer affirmatively. They are in the gym training, walk through the door and appear in the foyer. The rest of the fighters begin to spread out through As Talladas almost invisibly amongst the rest of Tinddaya.

― Cornelia: It’s here, it’s the same feeling -the dogs are restless. Cornelia goes down to the road and looks everywhere, walking towards the children’s granary.

― Nikoleta: It’s all right, she’s not here. Lázaro and I are going out to the road -they both meet Cornelia.

― Nélida: We are already in the hall, Salma, Roland, what about the combatants?

― Alejo: I’ll be right there -Jimena meets her brother and walks with him to the hall.

― Koiki: I’ll keep the child. The Teraghe`n is restless.

― Trisib: We don’t hear it anymore!

― Hajnalka: Be herrre -she says, her murmur echoing nearby. They search for her with their eyes-. Down herrre -Roland looks to the ground, in the reflection of the surface they can see the traveller walking among them. The rest look down as well.

― Roland: Welcome. As always surprising in your appearance. Won’t you join us? -he tilts his head to look for her eyes in the reflection.

― Haj: Not necessarrry -she looks very carefully at the combatants, standing just above the reflection of the two sisters, overlapping her figure with theirs-. Interrresting, grrreat warrriorrrs -she strokes their hair in the reflection, the women move into a defensive stance-. Ha!!!! -she lets out a loud laugh that echoes throughout the room-. Good girrrls -and her reflection fades.

― Joanna: What the fuck is wrong with that woman! -she exclaims, running her hand through her hair roughly.

― Nakano: She’s done something, I’m sure -she says, touching the ground, looking at the slabs on which the woman appeared.

― Alejo: She’s afraid of you! -he exclaims, smiling with his eyes-. Good! GOOD!!!!

― Rol: It is possible…

― Nél: -she looks puzzled- Koiki, is it true that she’s no good?

― Koi: I don’t know, she hides even from Teraghe`n.

― Uriel: Fuck! That was too short.

― Azazel: It’s left me exhausted and the Matrix completely inert.

― Rol: Gabriel?

― Gabriel: I’m fine, I’m just very tired. She has annulled everything, the psyche of that traveller is brutal.

― Jimena: Maybe she’s not a traveller -Alejo looks at his sister carefully.

― Ale: It’s true that those aren’t Tinddaya powers, tell me, is it a creature of the shadows, is it Mérida’s work? -Jimena is perplexed, Cornelia glares at him.

― Nél: Never mind, Alejo. It’s time to think about the next step, OK?

― Rol: Right. There is no room for discussion. We understand that it is paramoun to provide a defence.

Ω

Salma goes to the Matrix accompanied by Lázaro. She opens the door without knocking on the Matrix. Nikoleta is taking them down when Gabriel puts his helmet back on.

― Nikoleta: You can’t go in again!

― Salma: Hey, what are you doing? -she exclaims as she sees them with their helmets on, about to be suspended on the wires again-. You’re in no condition to do that.

― Uriel: She’s going to do something to show her power, we have to stop her first.

― Nik: -approaching Azazel- That’s crazy, you’re putting yourselves in danger.

― Gab: We have to find her! She wants us to find her!

― Aza: Jimena is right, if it’s not a traveller, it could be something extraordinary, think about it.

― Sal: What are you talking about?! She’s going to kill you! Get out of there, you can’t operate without authorization -she sentences.

― Gab: Leave us, Salma. We can find out things about her, we can be ahead and save As Talladas.

― Sal: I’m sorry -she says, approaching a small desk with a digital tablet, picks it up and stops the Matrix-. Communicator, Ekaitz.

― Ekaitz: Salma, what’s going on?

― Sal: To the Trisib’s room, you and a team.

In a few minutes they are taking their vitals and checking that everything is fine.

― Eka: Let’s stabilise them -Salma nods and Ekaitz places some gadgets on them that look like a double headband. He waits for the measurement-. There’s high activity, it’s as if she tried to knock you both out -Salma looks at them carefully, pondering.

― Sal: You’ve been looking for her without permission, that’s why you’re like this. You spotted her trying to get in and she knocked the barrier. If she couldn’t, it was because of the fighters, they caught her by surprise. Or am I wrong?

― Aza: No… we were tracking her, she was prowling around the Mine, we contained it, until we were overwhelmed.

― Sal: When you’ve recovered, we’ll talk about this together. Anything else you know? -They shake their heads.

Ω

Hajnalka stands in the centre of a vast, raw stone-walled space. Artificial lights flicker to disappear, in the darkness violent purplish flames rise and burn their way through all of the Hal-Saflieni halls. The blackness covers the walls with a spectral glow and the voices of past lives murmur in every corner, the figures of the dead are blurred, moving from one side to the other like thick shadows, trying to escape from the walls that hold their bodies.

Hajnalka remains unchanged. From the flames emerge the three ravens. They are dressed in black robes and a wide-brimmed hat, they walk with a low gesture of reverence, a sign of respect for the temple. They surround the woman. Each one carries a book from which hangs a small rosary. All three hold a writing pen.

They open the book, it is the same ritus (rite) of every dawn.

― Mercury: -with the quill resting on a blank sheet of paper- Welcome, War Master.

― Arsenic: Report back to us, Kennin ji.

― Radon: Talk.

Hajnalka closes her eyes, the flames beneath her feet fan, the letters around her body shudder like exalted smoke. The quills begin to write on their own on the paper, they read the deaths caused by the creature of chaos, they read what concerns the Tinddaya, the discord generated in the Mine.

The quills stop and the three ravens read carefully. They smile in satisfaction, Hajnalka fulfils their wishes. They close the books and with a half-smile they leave, retreating motionless. The flames, the voices and the figures disappear. Hajnalka stands in the centre of the room, waiting for the ravens to go further away.

There is something she has not let the ravens know, something that has not been written.

Ω

The mast of the boats is silhouetted against the moonlight reflecting on the water, the tinkling of the ropes clashes as they are swayed by the gentle breeze. The street is silent, barely a car passes by. Hajnalka stands in front of the house, standing still. Through the windows, the light shines on and the figures of the two women can be seen, talking to each other as they prepare dinner. The pale figure fades away, but on the wet asphalt you can see her reflection walking into the house. In the archway before the door, above the porch steps, hangs an omamori, which burns to ashes as the woman passes by.

Nakano is setting the table, as Joanna cooks, the frying pan with the food falls to the floor “CLAAANNK!!!”

― Nak: Jo, are you alright?! -she asks, dropping everything on the table and rushing towards the kitchen-. WAAAHHHH!!!!

The house is left empty.

The forest howls through the huge leaves of the thousand-year-old sugi, raindrops fall, but it is not rain, it is humidity that barely lets you breathe. The canopy of trees barely lets in the light of dawn, which ghostly passes through the fog banks of Shiratani Unsuikyo.

Among the huge roots, lying on the rocks, are the two combatants. They lie groggy on the ground, completely drenched in sweat, breathing hard, the battle to awaken is evident in their gestures, but they are gone, as if their wills were far away. They have a deathly appearance, something is running under their skin. A small trail of dried blood is evidence of the dragon’s bite; it has poisoned them. Hajnalka drags them to the river, the current keeps them under the surface drowning them, they convulse, releasing the air, they struggle, after a few seconds their bloodshot eyes open, they are fully awake, but without any will.

Hajnalka sheds her clothes, all the ideograms on her body are now exposed. She steps into the water up to her hips, followed by Joanna and Nakano, one on each side. The sound of the forest stops as Hajnalka closes her eyes. The combatants move their arms performing their magic VUUUUMMM!!! energy flies to crash into the pale body, VUUUUUUUUMMMMMMM!!!, one of the drawings on her body blur, the ink of the ideograms flows over the water blackening it VUUUMMMM!!! again and again, more and more intense, the fighters are in a state of deep alienation, their veins and muscles swell to the brink of bursting VUUUMMMM!!! the ideograms completely fall apart over her body and slide into the water VUUUMMMM!!!!! Hajnalka opens her eyes. The sisters are thrown off, crashing into the trees and falling unconscious in the moss and roots.

BBRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRR!!! a rattling sound upstream. Hajnalka turns and looks in the direction of the sound, waiting undeterred. The water recedes in for a few seconds, uncovering the rocks, it rises upstream until it disappears, the fish are struggling on the stones. A figure is drawn between the trees, coming down at full speed, a gigantic arm of water is thrown over the woman hitting her and throwing her into the void POOOOOOOOOOOOMMMMMMM!!!! Another blow, the water hand opens and from its centre emerges a paper seal with 7 kanji, the hand is stamped against Hajnalka, the paper burns against her skin leaving the letters on fire. AAAAAAAAAAAAAAHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!!!!! a guttural scream, a quick gesture of the woman who sentences the water hand by severing it NOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO!!!! is heard in the farthest distance of time. In the dark, rocky room Mulagua falls on his face, his hand is bleeding on the floor and with the other he painfully holds his mutilated arm. The arm of water bursts against the ground, returning the stream to its flow. Hajnalka sits up in a rage, she looks at the combatants, they have not enough level to break Mulagua’s seal, but the seal is not enough to stop her fury. Her debased eyes burn with flames, her fingers claws tear at flesh, RRAAAGGGGG!!!, the figure of the dragon emerges, flaying the skin. Its bare chest holds the scorched pattern of the Fayac.

AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH HHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!!!!!!!!!!!!! Her scream echoes throughout the island stopping the rivers and the tides of fog.

Ω

Footsteps echo down the corridor in a hurry. Orone opens the door with force, Mulagua is on the floor, his wound has been cauterised with black fire, but the pain is intense. Orone comes over and helps him to get back on his feet, grabbing him by the shoulder and dragging him along.

― Oro: Come on, in my workshop I have cork oak spider juice -Mulagua nods with a mumbled yes.

They both walk to the workshop, enter and Orone leaves him lying on a stone bench. He lifts the robe and looks at the wound, it is closed, a clean cut. From one of the shelves he takes a small flask. He reaches over and drops a couple of drops on Mulagua’s tongue. The man’s breathing relaxes.

― Oro: I have a right hand in cave cypress wood -he says as he takes the carved wooden limb out of a chest. He walks over to it and puts it on, measuring it, a bit big, adjust the size with some pins and fasten it with the straps-. Ready? -Mulagua nods. “Etturegh is Emûheḍ en temsé” (I call upon the evil being of hellfire) -from the wood roots grow deep into the flesh, Mulagua shudders-. Try to move your fingers -Mulagua moves them awkwardly-. In a couple of days you will be used to it.

― Mul: Thank you, Orone.

Ω

Kennin ji’s scream has shaken the Teraghe`n, among those most sensitive to it the sensation has been undeniable, something evil has been released. In the Mine there’s silent, almost all the Tinddaya have already left. Jimena is next to Cornelia, both of them are in the tree house, the candles suddenly go out and the dogs start barking and circling around. Alejo is in the gymnasium, hitting the bag, the shudder makes him lose his balance, he falls to the ground, he watches in bewilderment. Roland and Nélida were drinking wine on the balcony of the granary, leaning on the railing, they almost choke and quickly go down to the road. Lázaro is lying on the sofa playing on his mobile phone, he drops the phone on the floor and gets up, Nikoleta appears in his room with a frightened face, they both look out of the window. Salma is lying on a pouffe with her headphones on, listening to loud music with her eyes closed, she jumps to her feet and looks around.

Koiki picks up a couple of branches, the energy focuses between his fingers awakening a flame, he lays it on a stone and observes it. He closes his eyes. At the violet forest, Tanuk ik is right in front of him with his eyes wide open.

― Tanuk ik: The alchemists you call. Emergency is they to bring us to this side -Koiki nods and opens his eyes, walking quickly towards the mestizo’s granary.

Jimena and Cornelia see him from the house, they go down to meet him. The door of the gymnasium opens loudly, Alejo gets out and walks in search of his sister.

― Cornelia: Koiki, what did you see?

― Koiki: Urgent. Jimena, you have to go for the Alchemists.

― Jimena: Zéfiro and Arguestes will want no part of this war.

― Koi: They don’t have to participate, they just have to bring in the guardians.

― Cor: No shit, Hajnalka isn’t human -she looks at Koiki deeply, he holds her gaze-. You knew that.

― Koi: -nods- But we can’t do anything, only the alchemists can at this point. It is the thunder guardian who has been awakened. Kennin ji is among us, but she keeps a bond.

― Jim: -taking a deep breath- I’ll talk to them -Alejo arrives next to them.

― Alejo: Where are you going? -he asks, taking his sister by the hand.

― Jim: Zéfiro and Arguestes.

― Ale: Oh shit! That cunt is one of Moor’s war masters, motherfucker, I knew it… -he says hitting the ground-. But… where are you going, if those two don’t give a shit about anything!

Jimena breaks free of his grip, and walks towards the granary. She reaches the door, turns the knob in a peculiar configuration and the door opens onto a dreary, empty street. She walks through, closing the door behind her.

― Ale: They’re not coming, you’re wasting your time… And you, Koiki, you knew it, you knew it! -he says with a fierce look on his face-. We aren’t prepared because of you.

― Cor: -she steps in- There’s nothing to prepare against a son of Moor, Alejo -she pushes and separates him completely.

― Koi: I did what I had to do. If I had said so I would only have endangered the Mine.

― Ale: AAAHHH!!!!! -he screams out of his mind.

Jimena walks up the street, there is no one, yet there is a ghostly halo, the life of the inhabitants seems to resonate as if there were people in the houses, walking on the pavements. The sky is orange, but with a sad grey tone. Moss and grass grow over the few parked cars. The windows, some open, are dirty with rain and dirt, the curtains sway in the breeze. There are pigeons gurgling on the cornices and a cat stalking them. The houses are silhouetted against the mountains, tall and distant, with leafy valleys separating the village from the wilderness. There is a large canopy of trees visible at the end of the street, it looks like the town square.

Jimena arrives at the square, stops at the same corner. The vegetation has swallowed up the kiosk and most of the benches, but one of them is still completely visible, surrounded by pigeons eating next to the seated figure feeding them. It doesn’t flinch, just sniffs the air. It is somewhat superhuman, with reddish hair all over its body, its face is a cross between wolf and human, its eyes are amber, it is covered with a dirty, torn trench coat and old corduroy trousers tied with a rope.

― Werewolf: What are you doing here, Jimena?

― Jimena: Hello, Zéfiro. I’ve come to talk to you.

― Zéfiro: I can sense that. Come closer -Jimena walks towards him. Zéfiro inhales the woman’s scent, expanding his pupils.

― Jim: This is about a Moor’s war master, Kennin ji is awake.

― Zéf: Life comes and goes, Jimena. The calm you seek is unnatural, selfish. Whatever Kennin ji does, it will be no better or worse than what you did, the mestizos, the Empress or the ravens.

― Jim: I am not here to be lectured. Only to inform you that Tanuk ik is calling you to open the gates to the dream plane, from where the guardians can return Kennin ji to the abyss.

― Zéf: I heard you. You can leave.

― Jim: Is Arguestes still alive? -Zéfiro raises his head and howls. A deeper howl answers from the distance.

― Zéf: He’s not the same as the one you met, none of us are.

Arguestes’ run echoes as he approaches, the weight of his paws thunder against the tarmac. He is completely black, grey-haired, green-eyed, the face is much wilder, prominent teeth blurring the lip, the nose crooked. The hands are barely human, the torso is cheetah-like, and the large tail waves behind him. He wears no clothes, only a necklace, the same one that also hangs around his brother’s neck. He stops when he reaches the square, then takes a deep look at the mestizo and smells her.

― Jim: Hello, Arguestes.

― Arg: Mmmgrraammmr -a guttural, unintelligible sound comes out of his throat.

― Zéf: My brother says hello. And now that we’ve met, I think it’s time for you to go home.

― Jim: -looks at the necklace on both of them- I remember that necklace on Nereo and Viveka.

― Zéf: Don’t mention our parents. They won’t help you change our minds. Go away.

― Jim: Goodbye -she turns and walks back the way she came.

The two brothers watch her leave. Her scent fades as she walks through the door. Arguestes looks at his brother.

― Zéf: He wants us to help them bring the guardians to this plane.

― Arg: Gggrrmmmrmbbbrrr… -he growls approaching Zéfiro and rubbing his head against his.

― Zéf: That is their way, it is no longer our way.

Ω

In the forest the earth trembles, the master of war, Kennin ji, explodes his energy making the whole of the China sea rumble, he is propelled falling on Fukushima, the radioactive waste and the death’s atmosphere feed his essence. Its size increases, but the seal prevents its release, its body deforms, losing the shape of the dragon, the energy flows towards it BOOOOOOOOOOOOOO!!!!!!!! BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOMMMMM!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!! PRRRROOOOM MMMMMM!!!!!!! The power plant’s chimneys explode and spill out. The nuclear mushroom does not reach the sky, it is absorbed at full speed by the huge mass it is transforming into, the ground is crushed under the creature. It grows by the second, its arms multiply, embracing any source of energy, the electric towers are defeated towards Kennin ji rolling down the mountains, setting the forest on fire in its path. The black clouds swirl around, rumbling thunder and lightning crashing violently down on the war lord.

Ω

Radon is standing on the mound of Anta Grande looking up at the sky, all is quiet in the meadow, the raven watches a hedgehog eating beetles in the bushes. Mercury and Arsenic are inside. They have pulled down the roof to let the energy flow to the stars, they are waiting for Hajnalka’s arrival when a rattle shakes the three ravens. They rush outside.

― Radon: It’s free!!! Did you feel it?

― Mercury: -taking a deep breath- No… she’s still tied up, but not by us.

― Arsenic: What the hell, the antediluvians?

― Mer: -nods- It was them.

― Rad: It’s impossible!!!, did they do it to tie her up again?

― Ars: They can’t rule her! The mask of Cräpatos is in our power.

― Mer: Never mind -he says with a half-smile.

― Rad: What do you mean it doesn’t matter? We don’t know how to control it.

― Mer: The dragon is not free and Tanuk ik will have already communicated with the mestizos, they will be there to return Kennin ji to his place. The old ones are enemies of both us and the Tinddaya, they will cause as much chaos as possible, but they will not allow the freedom of the war master, they do not seek the destruction of the world, only our extinction.

― Ars: So you propose that we move to inaction? -Radon looks carefully at Mercury.

― Mer: On the contrary, we will attack all the Tinddaya that mobilise, but we will leave the high level ones untouched, they will be the ones to lock up Moor’s child.

― Rad: If we send any necromancer to the battlefield before a warlord, many of our people will die. Kennin ji will make no distinction.

― Mer: We will act from a distance.

― Rad: What if the mestizos fail, what if the seal of the antediluvians dissolves, who will return the war master to the other plane? Neither we, nor all the necromancers combined, have the power to imprison Kenninji when his fury is unleashed -he says fiercely-. He will come after us, seek out Faust’s essence, awaken him, and then the rest will follow.

― Mer: -twists the gesture contemptuously- You are afraid, brother, you are weak- -sentences contemptuously. He raises his arms, invoking his raven. Kaixo, Bele laguna! Dei egiten dizut! (greetings, my raven friend, I summon you) -and he takes flight disappearing.

Arsenic looks at his brother, a look of unease at Radon’s fear. He summons his raven and takes flight. Radon pulls the orb from his chest, throws it into the cavity of the mound, a vaporous, ancient figure emerges from within, walks towards the raven. Radon kneels.

― Rad: Mother -the figure looks at him.

― Mother: There is nothing to rule in a barren world.

― Rad: Thank you, mother -the figure vanishes and the orb returns to his hands-. Kaixo, Bele laguna! Dei egiten dizut! (greetings, my raven friend, I summon you) - it flies off into the sky.

Ω

Alejo stands next to Roland, Nélida and Salma in the meeting room, Salma is sitting on the edge of the armchair next to her sister. Alejo paces back and forth, looks out of the window and walks back into the room.

― Salma: We can start without her, we’re wasting time if they say no.

― Alejo: I know. But if they said yes, the modus would be totally different. Give her one more minute -he pauses as he feels his sister’s presence-. She’s here -a click and the knob turns, Jimena enters the room, everyone stands up.

― Roland: What did they say?

― Jimena: They won’t participate.

― Ale: FUCK MY LIFE!!! -he takes a pair of rubber balls out of his pocket and squeezes them frantically between his hands.

― Nél: So, shall we start the As Talladas’ defence and Kennin ji’s containment operation? -Jimena does not respond.

― Rol: -speaks into the communicator- To all Tinddaya, this is Roland, alert situation. We request all of Tinddaya’s adult presence in the Mine. The combatants who are ready will be under the orders of Joanna and Nakano. Closing communication.

― Nél: The combatants don’t give a signal.

― Ale: Where are they? They’re the ones who should be running all this.

― Sal: If they are not here, we will look for them. They are not essential, we can execute the plan together, we are all Tinddaya, we can’t do anything against a warlord. You’re clear on that, aren’t you? -Alejo clashes his fists together angrily.

― Jim: Mérida could contain it.

― Ale: Fuck Mérida and her fucking lineage! -he says, turning around and walking through the door.

― Sal: Roland, go on with the plan without the fighters, Nélida, you have to talk to Alejandra, she has to use the crystal skull. Alejo, come back, we are leaving for Fukushima.

― Nél: Alejandra didn’t care about us.

― Sal: Alejandra is the only Tinddaya who knows how to use her mother’s inventions.

― Nél: My goodness… all right.

Salma leaves with Alejo, they go to the textile warehouse, where the work suits are, and they both put on theirs. They walk hurriedly to the common room, and one after the other Tinddaya from all over the world enter. Roland welcomes them with instructions, and little by little the workshops end up filled up with Tinddaya working. Alejo is left standing by one of the doors, looking for the closest place to the nuclear power station.

― Ale: You’re not coming with me, yet -she looks at him crookedly-. Talk to Deer, the fighters are probably in danger -she opens the door and disappears.

― Sal: Holy shit… What a bastard! -opens communication with Deer, the Trisib and Lázaro.

― Deer: We’ll await instructions.

― Salma: Deer, the fighters are not giving a signal. Spark is the fastest tracker, I need you him to find them.

― Dee: No bother, he will.

― Sal: Thank you.

Salma walks through the door, the Trisib are on their feet, waiting.

― Uriel: We were waiting for this moment.

― Sal: The fighters are giving no sign, first them, then Kennin ji.

― Azazel: They can knock out our tracking, but I don’t understand why they should.

― Gabriel: -closes his eyes and they vibrate under his eyelids- They’re not hiding, they’re just not there, I can’t find them.

― Sal: They’re not there, what exactly does that mean… Have they been killed?

― Aza: Well, they are not there. If they were dead, they would be traceable.

― Sal: Let’s leave it to Deer and Spark -the three nod-. How are you doing to enter the Matrix? -all three put on their helmets and hover.

― Sal: You know what you have to do, hide it from the humans and don’t let it grow, but please don’t do it at the cost of your lives.

The three enter the Matrix and begin to operate. Salma comes out of the Matrix speaks into the communicator.

― Sal: Lázaro, Nikoleta. I’m on my way to the pilot’s room. Nikoleta, you’re the link between the Trisib and the work camp, stay with them.

― Nikoleta: Right away.

― Lázaro: I’m there now.

Salma arrives to the control room and all the pilots are in their control chairs, they are ready for action. Lázaro stands before them.

― Salma: Boys, girls, to the armchairs, get going, we are going to Japan, Lázaro will be your guide. We don’t know what we’re going to find, so give it your all, but the goal is to take care of the Tinddaya on the battlefield.

― Láz: Fins allà us heu d’anar? (That far you have to go?) You are putting yourselves at risk while I’m sitting here. It’s not fair.

― Sal: I can’t deal with this right now, I need you to control the drones better than ever. And please take care of us -she says with a worried look. He nods with determination. All the pilots are seated. The drones are activated and take off from different parts of the world heading for Japan. Lázaro’s drone takes off from the Mine and the machine soars like a cloud of birds silhouetted against the mountain.

Salma walks back to the hall. She opens the same door as Alejo, about 20 metres away is the mestizo, Alejo observes from the terrace of a building Kennin ji’s wingspan it is a giant mass of tentacles. She approaches him.

― Ale: In any of the library texts does it say how to kill a warlord?

― Sal: No.

― Ale: You can’t kill that with what we have…

― Sal: Not even with Alejandra’s crystal skull?

― Ale: No.

― Sal: So let’s die with dignity.

― Ale: We’re fucked…

The Almost-Shogunate of the Chaos Kaiju

It is a street with hardly any cars, of terraced houses but no luxuries, the outskirts of the city, illuminated by orange street lamps, at the end of the street you can see the orchard. The mountain looms on the other side, the tide of clouds trapped at the top. “TOC, TOC, TOC”, knocking on the wooden door, all silent. The light comes on, behind the curtain the young woman looks out surprised and shaking her head.

― Alejandra: -from the other side of the door- Nélida, please leave.

Nélida walks through the Mine, down to the drone room, everything is full of screens displaying the battlefield, there are many Tinddaya controlling the robots. Roland is standing in front of one of the screens.

Ω

Deer is sitting at the desk, in front of a tray full of letters cut out of magazines and newspapers. Spark is behind him, standing, watching his friend’s movements. They are in the basement, lit only by candlelight. Deer’s eyes are closed, randomly picking letters off the tray, but the movement of his hands is peculiar, slightly jerky, the letters are being dropped one after the other, in a row, creating a pair of words “Shiratani Unsuikyo Shinrin”. Spark taps Deer’s shoulder, waking him from his trance.

― Spark: What was it you saw? -he asks, translating the words on his mobile phone.

― Deer: A forest, a great big tree, water.

― Spark: Then we have them.

― Deer: Do you know him?

― Spa: Aye, it’s the forest that inspired Princess Mononoke. Kennin ji has been loosed there. It’s an ancient place; it has raised the power of the master, and the combatants’ along with it.

― Dee: Theirs? -he asks in surprise.

― Spa: Not by their own will. Come on.

They both walk through the door, appearing in the Mine. Spark picks up the tablet with the record of all the bridges set up, quickly scrolling through the directory. There is one at 2304-14 Miyanoura in Yakushima, they have found it,

― Spa: That’s our door.

The sliding door opens, it is a small wooden hut with large windows, it has tables inside, with several clients seated, the cook is a gentleman who serves and takes notes. Leaning in any corner there are travellers’ backpacks. Everyone seems suprised to see them, but dissimulate. Spark is wearing a hawaiian shirt and socks with sandals and Deer is wearing a tank top and shorts, the rest of them are warmle dressed and wearing hiking boots. Spark turns to the couple next to him.

― Spa: Is this good? -they nod. Spark reaches for a piece of sushi-. Mmm… it is good -he mumbles as he leaves the establishment.

― Dee: Sorry -he exclaims, pulling a few pounds out of his pocket and dropping them on the table. He comes out after his friend-. Here! Wait for me! -he exclaims as he sees him already running into the woods.

― Man of the restaurant: Tomodachi, matte kudasai (friend, please wait) -he exclaims, walking out of the door with his hand raised-. “Tihulan Tinddaya ayt Naga” -he says under the gaze of the two Welshmen- Where are you going? You can’t go into the forest like that -he speaks with a strong Japanese accent.

― Dee: We’re looking for two Tinddaya in danger; they’re in the forest.

― Man: You cannot go alone. There is a guide among the hikers, he will take you. Give me a second -the man comes in, talks to one of the diners, in a matter of a minute, they are both outside.

― Dee: Domo arigato (thank you very much).

― Guide: No problem. Tell me exactly where they are.

― Dee: They’re at a jump in the river, great big trees, a bank of wee stones…

― Guide: There is only one area of the river with these conditions, we can get there in an hour if we walk fast.

― Dee: There’s no time on us. Follow us, if you please -the three of them turn off the main road. Deer pulls a pair of pods out of his backpack, activates them and launches them, after a small cloud of smoke two individual flight drones appear-. They’re drones; they’ll shift up to a hundred kilos and they work on voice command. The straps go on like this -he says as he helps him put them on, Spark has already put on his one-. Drone 2, up ten feet -the machine lifts the man off the ground. Spark is already in the air-. There’s nothing to it: forward, up, down, faster, brake; say any word at all and it’ll take the order -the guide nods, still a little surprised.

― Spa: Come on. We’re losing time.

― Dee: Have patience; we’re earning it with him here. Take the backpack. I’ll wait for you on the far side -Spark nods with an imperceptible gesture of discomfort at the fact of not being accompanied by his friend.

The men fly through the forest. Deer heads back to the restaurant. He stands in the doorway listening to the intercom.

The drones fly over the river towards its source, only the murmur of the water can be heard, about 20 minutes have passed when some coloured spots are drawn behind the leaves.

― Spa: There!

It’s them, they are half-submerged in the water, they are very weak. The drones stop a couple of metres away, the men go down to the ground. Spark approaches them, puts his ear to their chest, they are throbbing slowly, their breathing is very subtle. He turns and heads towards the guide.

― Spa: Thank you. You’ll have to go back. Drone 2, to origin -the drone lifts off with the guide away from the site-. Communication with Deer.

― Dee: Spark?

― Spa: I have them.

― Dee: I’m on my way -he puts the knob on, pushes the sliding door and appears in the hall of the Mine-. I’m coming.

Spark takes another capsule out of the backpack, drops it. A door frame with a door leaf appears. He waits for the indicator to light up, when it’s green, he pushes the blade and it opens into the Mine. Deer crosses to the other side to help bring the women in, they are both dragged inside.

― Dee: Communicate with Ekaitz.

― Ekaitz: Deer!

― Dee: They’re here. Bring help.

― Eka: Right away.

A moment later a team of doctors attend the women. They are lifted onto floating stretchers and taken to a room. The news that they have been found reaches everyone. Nélida arrives to the intensive care ward.

― Nél: How are they?

― Eka: Very weak, they have been poisoned, Kennin ji has bitten them. They show similar signs to those the Trisib suffered when trying to fight Hajnalka. The composition of the poison is sequenced, the leukodrones are currently purifying their bodies.

― Nél: Keep an eye on them, please -she turns and addresses Spark and Deer-. Have you spoken to them?

― Spa: Chan eil guth ri ràdh! (not a word).

― Nél: There was nothing next to them?

― Nél: I don’t doubt it, Spark. Thank you both for finding them.

― Dee: There’s nothing to thank us for. What do we do now?

― Nél: You can stay here or go to the battlefield with Alejo and Salma and fight the beast. It’s up to you.

― Dee: We’ll away with them.

― Nél: Put on a protective suit.

― Spa: There’s no need.

― Nél: Suit up or stay -she sentences, Spark nods.

They both rush to the suit storage. They get dressed, pressing a button on their wrists, the suit fits their bodies and is activated. They get to the common room, the nearest door is the same one used by Alejo and Salma. They walk through. Just outside Spark presses the button on his wrist and removes the suit.

― Dee: You’ll have to wear it. Kennin ji can do us harm.

― Spa: They’re not built to stand up to a warlord -he replies crudely. Deer looks down.

― Dee: Communicate with Salma.

― Sal: Deer, how are they?

― Sal: Thanks guys. I’m droping you the location.

― Dee: Away we go -the location reaches the suit, on the screen it shows the distance travelled by the woman.

They are at 1200 metres, the figure of the beast is huge, a very subtle bubble is visible to Tinddaya eyes, it has been created by the Trisib with the Matrix. The assault drones begin to arrive, flying over the heads of the Scots on their way to their comrades. Alejo and Salma wait on a small hillock, next to them is Arvada, the next level combatant after Joanna and Nakano. Deer and Spark arrive next to them. The drones are hovering in the air, firing beams of light that strike the beast’s arms. Groups of combatats positioned around the beast make defensive moves.

― Arvada: We can’t do more than this to use the defence against necromancers. If we do anything else, we risk to increase their power.

― Alejo: Keep it up.

― Salma: Roland, can you give us a iew of the centre of the beast?

― Roland: Drone number 56, view of the chest -the drone takes position, the image is clear. There is a seal on which the energy is concentrated. Everyone sees the image on the suit screen.

― Arvada: It is a necromancer Kanji, its stroke is clear, but I can’t identify it.

― Ale: Nélida, can you contrast that ideogram?

― Nél: Yes.

― Spa: He’s containing it, not mastering it. Yon kanji is neither here nor there now.

― Sal: Are you sure?

― Nél: You are right. The ideogram is not Japanese, the most similar one belongs to the Hongshan period. It’s not a raven that did that.

― Jim: They are not helping us, they just avoid catastrophe.

― Ale: There will be an actual catastrophe if they don’t try a little harder.

― Sal: Koiki -talk to the sensitive who is still in the Mine with all his disciples.

― Koiki: Our Teraghe`n concentrates on Kennin ji, but from here we can only contain the barrier.

― Ale: Deer, how many doors do you have?

― Dee: One.

― Ale: Open it -Deer pulls out the capsule, which transforms into a door-. Koiki, everyone cross to this side -Koiki looks for the nearest door, it’s in one of the granaries, he places the knob, crosses with his disciples and joins the rest. They are all perplexed when they see the size of the beast, it must be at least 30 metres long-. Koiki, organize your people, let’s go!

― Koi: We will stand behind the fighters, they will project our energy.

There are several hundred sensitives, running in haste towards the groups of combatants surrounding the beast. A few minutes later they are all ready. They place their hands on the shoulders of the combatants and energy flows into their hands.

― Koi: Arvada, ask your people to let their hands move at the will of the Teraghe`n.

― Arv: Combatants, we are going inoperative, let the energy of the sensitives direct you.

The hands of the combatants shine with a different glow, they draw figures in the air, the light condenses and flows slowly towards Kennin ji, the beams of light collide with the beast, the darkness loses speed, stops growing.

― Dee: They’re doing it!!!! -he exclaims excitedly.

― Ale: Salma, what do we do to lock him up?

― Sal: I don’t know.

― Spa: If we had Cräpatos’ mask we could do something.

BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM MMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM!!!!! One explosion after another, the group is thrown to the ground, Arvada generates a dome above them. The explosions are destroying the drones, the force field of the Trisib and are also breaking the light beams of the sensitives, the fighters fall to the ground protecting the others, BOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMMM! Numerous black trails fly like arrows, some break through the containment barrier, others hurl darkness at kennin ji making it bigger.

― Ale: THEY ARE NIGROMANTS!!! ROLAND, NÉLIDA, LÁZARO!!! ATTACK!!!! ARVADA, HALF TO CONTAIN, HALF TO ATTACK!!! THRETWINS, HOLD THE TARGET!!!!

Drones fly at supersonic speed. FUUUUUUHH!!!, some are shot down, others knock out many necromancers. The movements of the fighters cause some necromancers to fall to the ground inert. Kennin ji continues to grow in virulence.

― Nik: Alejo, the Trisib are boiling, they’re going to explode! -the three brothers hang from the straps that suspend them in the air, having strong spasms. Nikoleta lowers the temperature in the room, it is freezing, but they are sweating, she unzips their immersion suits. There are two doctors checking the monitors next to her.

― Galen 1: 2 minutes before cardiac arrest!

― Nik: NOOOO!!!! ALLEJO!!! ORDER IT TO STOP! -she shouts, looking at the ceiling.

― Female voice: Nélida, give me access to the door before the beast.

― Nél: Done -she sends them.

Alejandra appears next to them. Cornelia rushes up to them.

Nélida, tell your people to close their eyes and stop operating, even those in the Mine. There can be no interference.

The Matrix bubble disappears, sensitives and fighters stand still, drones fall to the ground and at last, everyone closes their eyes. The trails of black shadows continue to cast their magic now against the beast that continues to grow exaggerated, its arms stretch even further, devouring even necromancers that get too close. Alejandra opens the box in her hands, there is a pendant with a cat skull carved in quartz, she places it around her neck. AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAHHHHHHHH!!!! A beam of light blinds everything along with the girl’s reverberating scream. The ground is littered with dead necromancers, their burnt bodies lie lifeless and disfigured as they hit the ground. Kennin ji is much smaller now, the mass of darkness starts to get disfigured, the dragon’s face turns unrecognisable. Alejandra lies unconscious, on the floor in an unnatural position. Cornelia kicks open the door and grabs the girl by the arms.

― Cor: Jimena, take her by the legs -both women drag the girl through the door. On the other side is Nélida with a team of doctors. They take care of Alejandra. The door closes.

The rest look on in awe.

The three ravens observe the action from a distance. They are standing on the roof of an abandoned building. Radon is the only one pacing restlessly.

― Radon: If we use the mask now, we can lock it up.

― Mercury: No change of mind.

― Rad: They’ve just killed all the necromancers! The crystal skull has broken the seal of Kennin ji!!!! We don’t know what the antediluvians are going to do.

― Mer: No change of mind I said -he glares at him, Arsenic looks at him with a sly smile.

― Rad: We’re going to regret this -he stands still behind them, biting his lips.

There is a moment of silence, waiting as they watch Kennin ji slowly return to its original form. The drones surround it.

― Spark: Lázaro, picture of the dragon’s chest -the drone moves, the image focuses on the black mass. Spark throws his hands to his head.

― Alejo: FFFUCKING HELL!!! It doesn’t have it anymore. FUCK!!!

GOOOOOONNNNGGGGG!!!! The sound of a metallic thunder echoes as the dragon regains form GOOOOOOOOOONNNNNNGGGGGG!!!! Arvada signals to the fighters, they throw their restraint pushed by the hands of the sensitives. GOOOOONNNNNNNGGGGGGG!!!! The dome created by the Trisib from the Matrix is shattering, falling like vanishing crystals. The dragon figure recomposes itself GOONG!!!!! The air around them is almost opaque darkness, it is hard to breathe, the dragon remains standing, it looks around at each of them until its gaze stops on Alejo. The dragon makes a hand gesture BROOOOOOOOOOM!!!! a black bolt of lightning falls before him and becomes his halberd. It raises it to hurl it at the mestizo.

― Salma: Laazaroo!!! C’MONNNNNNNN! -the drones launch their full arsenal at the beast, but a fraction before touching the dragon the attack is reduced to dust.

― Lázaro: NOOOO!!!!

The dragon launches the beam that shoots towards the mestizo TAAAAMMMMMMMM!!!! The beam slows suspended in the air, a circle of light forms in front of the spear and upon collision KLIIINNNKKK!!! the energy dissipates in all directions. The air is filled with perfect geometries and other broken luminescent figures, TAMMM!!! two silhouettes appear on the dragon’s flanks, Zéfiro and Arguestes close to the beast, they raise their hands and the halo of their arms remains visible while they keep moving, they seem to have four arms. From each of their palms emerge four gyres that are released and stop around the dragon. At each of the gates appear the guardians, a panther warrior, a firebird, a water serpent, a wind equine, a bronze gryphon, a blind wolf and an elephant man. Tanuk ik steps into the real world from the world of dreams. Kennin ji bites down, consumed by anger.

― Mercury: NOW!!!!

Arsenic flies pointing like an arrow towards the dragon, the trail of darkness catches everyone by surprise, Radon is surprised to be unaware of the order. The crystal vial with the essence of the bat shatters over the dragon; giant wings rise up behind it, RAAAAAAAAAAAHHHHHHHHHHHHH!!!! The dragon’s fusion with the bat swoops down on Tanuk Ik. Radon turned into darkness pierces Mercury’s body, snatching the mask of Cräpatos from him.

― Mercury: NO!!!! -flies after his brother.

Radon forcefully throws the mask onto the beast. TUAAAAAAAAAAAAH!!!! the beast falls to the ground, severed. Tanuk Ik approaches the two war masters, seizing and taking them with him into the dream world. The light fades.

All present approach the place, watching with surprise and attention. The alchemists open a portal and cross it before anyone else can come any closer. They disappear. Arsenic flies back to Mercury, Radon flies after him under the watchful eyes of all the Tinddaya.

― Sal: To all injured Tinddaya in Fukushima, mark your location on the suit indicator, help is on the way. To the medical teams at the Mine, immediate attention.

― Arvada: The wounded are accompanied. What do we do with the necromancer survivors?

― Ale: You make that decision on your own?

― Sal: The decision is made by all of us, which is the one of saving lives. You are an imbecile but not a murderer.

― Ale: Glad to hear it.

The Mine is a coming and going of Tinddaya. Salma awaits by the door and Nélida on the other side of it while Alejo helps the rest of the teams on the ground with Deer and Spark.

Fukushima is shattered, the Matrix dome disappears. No sign of the Tinddaya remains.

Ω

The next day the air in the Mine is quiet from exhaustion. During the day there has been no lecture or investigation of any kind, everyone is waiting for further instructions on how to proceed after Kennin Ji’s attack. At seven o’clock in the evening the door to the meeting room is half open, there are people inside.

The same figures as always. There is some tension because of the casualties and the imbalance caused, they don’t have the answers needed to avoid another situation like this.

― Roland: There have been 7 Tinddaya casualties from the raven attack, 4 during the Kennin ji expansion. On the raven side, there are 57 dead and located after the use of the crista skull, 3 of them recovered and are now alive under guard protection outside the Mine.

The sisters are recovering and the circumstances show that their power is far greater than expected. Their presence here is a relief. On the other hand, we have Alejandra, who is still recovering in the care unit. We understand that her participation is fortuitous and that she will not come back to offer her help.

With regard to our proposals for dealing with the situation, despite the discrepancies, I believe that they have been good proposals, whether they were right or wrong. Alejo’s mistrust has served to provide us a foresight that we now quantify as essential. That said, we cannot forget that the necromancers, both the antediluvians and the ravens, are always ahead of us, and without the alchemists, we would have had no chance against the war master.

― Nélida: What we want to convey is that we are here to tell the story, whatever the reason. We don’t have the tools for everything that it is to come. And it is our togetherness and commitment to protect each other that has saved us.

I know that we all want to contribute, that we all have more than enough capacity to deal with different situations, but this war has brought us into conflict, it has highlighted the differences between us. I think it is essential to be clear about how we want to act when a dangerous situation like this arises. And now we know better than ever that we are a family and we cannot afford to expose and damage it.

― Uriel: We and the Matrix have reached our limits. Without all of you together it would not have been possible -he winks at Nikoleta.

― Salma: No one would have been able to do anything without the others, and even if we were united, this goes beyond us. We have to be humble and recognise our limits. I found myself disarmed, but ready to do anything for you. Thank you -she lets out a sigh, she is nervous.

― Azazel: Maybe so -he says, looking at the ground and rubbing his eyes-. It wasn’t right to believe that we could do everything by ourselves.

― Alejo: Nah…! I’m the one who’s redundant here. It’s time to replace my position -he looks at those present and turns his face away without losing his pride.

― Jimena: -looks up- We’re redundant -Spark approaches her, kisses her on the cheek, she gives him a kind look, he leaves the meeting. Deer is still in it.

― Deer: We all do the best we can.

― Cornelia: I think that’s enough for today, it’s time to rest -they all nod and slowly leave the room.

Ω

Alejo is sitting on the bleachers of the gym, next to him there is a basket full of tennis balls that he throws one after the other against the wall. Zaaaass! He throws the last one and kicks the basket, denting it. He gets up and goes to the door, on the tablet he selects where to open the knob. He opens it into a stairwell, up the stairs he hears a kettle boiling and the scent of tea, indie folk music playing, he climbs up and enters the kitchen.

― Alejo: Hi! Deeeer, Spaaaark -footsteps sound alerted by the voices. Deer arrives.

― Deer: Alejo, what has you here? Has something happened? -Spark arrives, doesn’t pay much attention, walks over to the kettle, takes it off the stove, takes out a third cup and pours the tea, putting it on the table. He sits down.

― Ale: I’m here to talk.

― Spark: The answer is yes.

― Ale: Fuck, you’re good, intuition, certainty or one of the gadgets?

― Spa: With you it’s all certainties -he brings him the sugar, Alejo puts in 5 spoonfuls.

― Dee: HA! -Alejo returns a cautious look. Don’t be taking offence; one of your virtues is that you see it coming. That’s a fine thing. You’re plain as day, sweet heart.

― Ale: I want you to take my sister’s and my occupations. After this, it is clear that we are not what everyone expected -Spark observes nonchalantly.

― Dee: It’s yourselves that are the mestizos; it’s you that give this project its worth.

― Dee: It’s not a question of standing out of it, but of taking part another way, maybe.

― Ale: Ah! -he lets out a sigh-, I was going to insist, but I see you’ve made up your minds.

― Spa: We’ll be your substitutes. If Jimena agrees, let Salma take her place.

― Ale: And Cornelia?

― Spa: Cornelia’s what we need, right enough, to keep the lot of us together -Alejo looks at Deer with some doubt, Deer nods with a tight smile.

― Ale: All right. If you don’t mind, I’ll have my tea before I go.

― Dee: We’ll away outside -Deer grabs the two cups and the speaker, Spark gets up and follows him.

The three of them go out into the garden, it is drizzling but they can see the meadow, the cows in the distance, and the huge cliffs that look like giants escaping from the earth and plunging into the sea. Alejo lets out an involuntary sigh after taking a sip from his cup, he is intoxicated by the beauty of the landscape.

― Ale: I didn’t know you lived in such a breathtaking place.

― Dee: Chan fhaca tu an rud as fheàrr (you haven’t seen the best of it) -he says with a smile, pulls out his phone and searches the playlist, turns up the volume on the speaker and the music starts playing, Spark stands still looking at the horizon, but moves his foot. Deer moves his lips as he sits at the window. Alejo breathes a sigh of relief.

“Happiness hit her like a train on a track

Coming towards her stuck still no turning back

She hid around corners and she hid under beds

She killed it with kisses and from it she fled

With every bubble she sank with her drink

And washed it away down the kitchen sink

The dog days are over… The dog days are done

The horses are coming

So you better run”

…

Ω

Jimena is completely underwater, with only her nose showing, her arms are outstretched and her legs spread out on the rocks in a shallow current. She’s speaking alone in her thoughts.

― Jimena: Have you seen it? Once again we, the mestizos with nothing to offer, had to be saved by the Alchemists. Are you proud to be the only mestizo capable of everything… Why have you done all this? What is your plan. .. - she goes from looking at the lights that the water forms over her eyes to see the garden, it smells of jasmine, the light is filtering through the leaves of the trees swaying in the breeze, she is sitted on a bench-. Nakano and Joanna are sitting on a sofa, Nélida is in front of them, it is a room similar to a flat, with a television, a table, a small kitchenette and a door leading to a room with two monitored beds. The girl is leaning out of the window, her mother is watching TV. She hasn’t been taught by Koiki for a few days, although she has been accompanied by one of his disciples. ― Koi: Let’s get on with it.

They both walk up to the riverbank, to a space where there are benches to sit on, where it’s quite hard to hear anything except the murmur of the mountain.

Everything is dark, there is a white mist with purplish reflections, the wind feels icy, the humidity floats in the air, the saltpetre can be breathed in the Añana valley. The three ravens are on one of the salt terraces. Mercury is standing, looking down at the ground, Arsenic is kneeling over Radon´s neck, holding his head under the salt water, his whole body is covered with it. Radon struggles to breath.

― Mercury: Wrong… wrong… very wrong… Radon. You have rebelled against the decision of the ravens, and no one, absolutely no one disregards my orders. You will die at the hands of our lord Gaueko -with a vicious smile he begins to invoke in Basque-. Deitzen zaitut, Gaueko, iluntasunaren jauna, deabru eta sorgin, arimen segatzailea… (I invoke you Gaueko, lord of darkness, demons and witches, reaper of souls…).

― Arsenic: -he looks at him with bewilderment- Will you do it? Mercury, I thought we were only going to scare him.

― Mercury: HAHAHAHAHA!!!! Scare him?, no brother, he has to pay with his sacrifice, or do you want to be the one to redeem his guilt?

― Arsenic: EZ!!! (no!) -he exclaims, shaking his head and plunging Radon deeper into the brine.

― Mer: Gaua gauekoentzat eta eguna egunekoentzat (night for those of the night and day for those of the day).

The salt crystals begin to shatter at Mercury’s words, there’s luminescence coming from somewhere far under the water, barely 20 centimetres deep, but the light and silhouettes of incubi are drawn in the reflection. The arms of the creatures of the underworld reach out towards Radon’s body, who is already barely twitching.

BRROOMMMM!!!! BRROOMMMM!!!! Several shocks shake the piles of the salt terraces, water splashes from one side to the other, Mercury and Arsenic turn around, BROOM!!! from the heights of the valley appear two luminaries of green fire, they are the Zezengorris.

― Radon: MERCURY!!!! STOP!!!! IS THE LADY OF ANBOTO!!!!

― Mercury: Mother has come to contemplate the death of the traitor -he turns and continues to invoke the Gaueko.

A third luminary appears between the two bulls of green fire, a woman, the same one who spoke to Radon. Her figure disappears to materialise between the ravens, her essence pushes Mercury away, who falls to another terrace, Arsenic falls rolling in terror under the gaze of the deity. The woman makes a gesture with her hand that pulls Radon out of the water completely, with the other hand she crystallises the water preventing the wolf demon from entering, the light of hell dissipates and the incubi disappear. The woman gives Radon a sharp blow to the chest. UGGGGGGGGHHHHHH!!! he spills all the swallowed water and breathes again. She leaves him on a rock carefully an then she disappears, and with her the green fire bulls.

― Ars: Radon… Are you okay? -he asks, frightened.

― Rad: Mo…the..rr fu…cke..r. LE..AVE..ME..A..LONE!!!

― Ars: He did it, I didn’t want to!

― Mer: -Mercury approaches them and gives Arsenic a kick in the belly- the lady saved you this time, but she won’t do it anymore, Radon. Rest assured -Mercury summons his raven and disappears.

― Ars: Let’s go home… -he mumbles, doubled over by the blow.

― Rad: You… you are not my home. Go away… -he loses consciousness and lies on the stone.

Arsenicus watches him as his gaze shows a look of guilt and fear. He summons his raven and disappears.

Ω

The corridors are silent, torches illuminate the sharp stone. Dampness condenses on the ceiling. There are a few canvases hanging on the walls next to crude paintings made by hand. The paintings commemorate the great milestones of the three antediluvians. Mulagua walks with an unbalanced sway, Kennin ji’s attack has not only cost him his hand. He reaches the main gate and whistles. A whistle is returned from within. He steps in front of his lords. The three heads peek out.

― Mulagua: Tamaraggwa Wayota (greetings demons) -he says in a broken voice. The child of Moor has fallen. The alchemists have intervened, opening portals to the dream world and bringing the guardians.

― Wayota 1: The mask…

― Mul: The mask is in Tinddaya’s hands.

― Wayota 2: The ravens…

― Mul: My Lords, the mother has intervened, they are disputing the leadership among themselves. It seems that the three ravens will soon split.

― Wayota 3: The Lady of Anboto… offers sacrifices, go to the Mariurrika Kobea.

― Mul: Be it, my lords, let the lady decide her fate.

Mulagua leaves the antediluvians’ living quarters. He walks through the corridors until he gets to a cage containing people behind bars, malnourished and in terrible conditions. He picks up three people and shackles them with the rings, they try to struggle and scream but they are barely strong enough. Orone appears out of the darkness.

― Orone: Where are you going, brother?

― Mulagua: To the lands of the Lady of Anboto. I must offer a sacrifice to the Yamnaya to thank them for their intervention.

― Oro: Let me come with you. On the way we’ll hunt high-level Tinddaya, and that’s what we’ll offer her.

The sun is blazing down the hillside, all the people are wearing short-sleeved shirts, caps and sunglasses. The tourists are wearing socks and their reddish skin tone gives them away. Everything is full of stalls with different products for sale. Fruit and vegetables, fabrics, antiques, furniture, toys, clothes, many things are second-hand. Everything is cheap and the bet is on who can make the best purchase for no more than 10€. Salma, Nikoleta and Lázaro walk around the flea market in Guardamar looking at everything.

― Nikoleta: Sunny Mummy, I’m going to melt. Stop for a moment and get the sunscreen -she says, taking the cream out of Lázaro’s backpack.

― Lázaro: Posa’m una miquigua (give me a little bit), I’ve put my hands in the drawer full of ninots (dolls) and they feel dirty.

― Salma: You’ve lost the bet, això és de ser inútil (you’re stupid).

― Lázaro: What are you talking about, drunky?! -he says, throwing his hands to his head-. 7€ for the GoldenEye for Nintendo 64 and the 80’s Skeleton figure.

― Salma: Let’s see, that doll has no blister pack, it’s missing some of the paint and the cartridge is almost burnt. Spend the money well -she says, rubbing her thumb and forefinger repeatedly.

― Nik: Also, if you don’t have the Nintendo 64 and you’ve only seen Netflix’s Masters of the Universe.

― Láz: What do you care if I want to be a young eighties guy, I recycle -he says annoyed. I haven’t said anything about the Sade vinyls you’ve bought, or the ukulele you’ve picked up even though you don’t know how to play it, and you’ve also gone over budget!

― Nik: Ouch, you get miffed so fast. Miffed!

― Láz: Puufffffff… you’re going to walk home.

― Nik: Sorryyyyyyyy…

While they are arguing, Salma goes to the toy stall and buys the Nintendo 64. Nikoleta sees her and runs up to her in surprise.

― Nik: What about me? I want the bomber jacket we’ve seen.

― Sal: Pos ala! (Then) Take out your card, I’ll buy it for you, but you pay for it, ha ha ha ha ha ha ha ha…

― Nik: Can we give you a present?

― Sal: Mmmmmh, have you seen the stall that had a cork full of badges. And it didn’t have a single one of them repeated…

― Láz: Which ones do you want?

― Sal: Which ones? -she says squinting her eyes. ALL OF THEM!!!! -they all laugh.

Ω

The silence is deafening, the village is barely a memory of what it was. Alejo walks slowly, looking from landmark to landmark, stopping at every detail he remembers from his last visit to Nereo and Viveka’s village. Jimena watches calmly, keeping pace with her brother, the previous time there was no time to recall a single moment of their childhood summers. The memories seem to be stuck somewhere far away, but every object, every façade, rings in her memory bringing back moments that seem to be from another life.

― Alejo: Will they be at the square?

― Jimena: They know we’re coming -they walk until they reach the end of the pavement leading to the square.

― Ale: Fuck! It’s like a forest in here -look at the huge trees that have grown up, hiding the whole space.

― Jim: They’re not there -she says, pointing to a bench on which there is not a single leaf. Alejo looks at it.

― Ale: Shall we sit there and wait? -she nods.

Both of them remain seated on the bench. Minutes pass, they don’t say a word until the sound of a ball makes them turn their heads towards the other corner of the square. There is a group of children throwing the ball back and forth, shouting like crazy, having fun. “Pass it! NOoo, not him, me! What are you doing, we’re going to lose! HAHAHAHAHAHA!!!” They are laughing non-stop. One of them breaks away without anyone seeing him while he approaches the fountain, fills three small balloons and throws them at his friends making a bullseye, “WOAAAAAAH!!! Start praying, ’cause if i get you…!!! They all leave the ball aside, approach the fountain and the game turns into a battle of balloons, everyone is soaked with water, their laughter and joy fills the square. Jimena and Alejo watch them without batting an eyelid, with a gesture somewhere between fascinated and melancholy. Two of the children have pointed ears and flat noses, they have hair on their bodies. One is blond and the other brown. There is also another boy, one throwing the balloons with a sulky face, and a girl who jumps in front of everyone to get more balloons and enjoy the water.”

The image gradually fades, the echoes are the last thing there’s heard in the square. They both turn around looking for the creator of the reverie. Zéfiro is a few steps away from them with Arguestes, they haven’t heard them approaching, they both get up.

― Zéfiro: It is one of the most beautiful memories we have of those summers together.

― Jimena: -wipes a tear from her cheek- Thank you.

― Alejo: Ughhh… that’s something… -he sighs, looking at his sister.

― Zéf: Arguestes and I thank you for your visit. I imagine you have a little speech prepared, but it is not necessary.

― Ale: On the contrary, we come only with a “Thanks”.

― Jim: Without your help…

― Zéf: We know -Arguestes makes a grimace that looks like a smile.

― Arg: AHamNaJreaaaGmmlt… -he makes unintelligible sounds, both look at him blankly.

― Zéf: He’s happy to see you, take care.

― Ale: I am very happy to see you -he approaches Arguestes and offers him his hands, Arguestes with some misgivings raises his deformed hands, Alejo takes them firmly-. I don’t know how you were able to dodge all my balloons. You owe me a rematch.

― Arg: Jjjrraaa -he lets out what sounds like a laugh, his eyes light up. Jimena walks over to him and strokes his face.

― Jim: The door is open. Always -she pats Zéfiro’s shoulder and turns away.

― Ale: -he pats Zéfiro on the chest- See you soon.

― Zéf: -his gesture changes when he touches Alejo, it has been many years since the last time someone touched him- Yes.

Both mestizos walk back. They do not turn around. The alchemists watch them go until they disappear. It is curious, but for a few minutes time has not only stopped, it has gone back 40 years.

The End

Addenda

The Alchemist’s Sons

I still recall those years of my childhood with fair clearness. I was a girl like any other. My parents were two pleasant people, a happy couple who lived in a simple village such as there are near any city.

An old village far from the great city, with a single dirt road opening into that leafy valley, where the inhabitants scarcely numbered six hundred.

Living there had its good and its bad. There were children of my own age who seemed cousins rather than friends, and the neighbours, of course, we called aunt and uncle. To be able to run from house to house is a thing that fills one with happiness; there are no borders…

What we longed for, and what weighed upon us deeply, was the one thing the television showed us daily and that we ourselves enjoyed but twice a year: the cinema, the fair, a walk through the city with an ice cream… The world we were shown was truly a different one from the world we lived in. To leave the village was the event of the year, and the truth is that an outing every two or three months would have suited us better.

But I must own that we wanted for nothing; more than that, the magic one breathed in the village did not float over the great city. To be honest, I do not believe that growing up in the city would have been as comforting as growing up in Lagredo. Now that I remember it from a distance, the truly indescribable thing was the surprise that any day might bring, living among those streets.

My parents used to say that one day we would go back to the capital, where they had grown up. Unhappily they were both dismissed, and could find no work after the economic crisis that was scourging the country. I remember them saying that there had never been so many people without employment and without money for food. With no funds and no subsidy, they could not meet the daily costs of the family; with the flat repossessed, and with the little our grandparents were able to lend us, they settled their accounts and we came to the village when I was but a year old.

In Lagredo nothing was needed but respect and goodwill. The houses stood there for whoever cared to live in them. Tilling, planting and raising livestock allowed the inhabitants to live without want. And the surplus that could not be stored was sold, giving the village a small income shared out among all the neighbours.

Now, after so many years in the city, I miss those times when living was a very different matter. The days were not flooded with stress, with pollution, with strangers, and with that magnanimous money which dirties everything.

The village where I was raised was truly special, not only for its natural beauty, nor for its quiet, nor for its people, but for the magic one breathed there.

I remember his face, the broken nose, his honey-coloured eyes, the look severe but kindly. The grey hair, the strong and weathered hands, his mysterious smile and an unending affection for every soul in the village. His name was Nereo, though we all knew him as the Alchemist.

His wife was a very beautiful woman, with olive-green eyes, a freckled complexion and hair as red as the evening; her name was Viveka. She knew nothing of the practice of magic, but she possessed a human depth capable of searching out the most secret thing with no more than a meeting of eyes.

The couple lived in a house set apart from the rest, a large old dwelling where he kept every implement his craft required. Through the corridors of that enormous house we ran back and forth more times than could be counted. Two events in particular remain engraved upon my memory.

Ω

The first of them happened on an ordinary afternoon. Three of us girls were skipping rope and four of the boys were playing at marbles. Shouting, laughing, letting the merry hours go by, breathless, until the late night of that summer should come.

We had grown tired of playing always at the same thing, so we decided to sit in the doorway of my house and wait for some amusement to occur to us. My mother, who was about the kitchen, came to look when she no longer heard our voices.

― What are you all doing sitting there? Have you tired of playing already?

― No, the thing is we are inventing a new game… but nothing comes to us -said one of the boys.

― Well now… then you must put your backs into it, and so, when you come to play it, it will be the more amusing. But I believe that if you come inside and sit at the table over a good piece of bread and chocolate, the ideas will come swiftly to mind.

We all accepted the invitation and sat down at the table. My mother set out chocolate, sweet bread, pieces of apple and a glass of milk for each guest. She smiled to see us so eager and so happy.

― I must go down to Benisa’s house. If you want more, it is on the second shelf, at your own height. I shall be back in a while. Agreed, youngsters? -we all nodded.

My mother went out and left us alone. Neither she nor we suspected that the afternoon would end in a maddening piece of mischief. Rarely did we quarrel, and rarer still did we commit any outrage. I could not say what wire crossed in us, nor why I let myself be carried along so. Giacomo rose from the table under the watchful eye of the rest and went to the corner cabinet where my mother’s collection of porcelain ballerinas stood.

They were true delicacies that she loved to look upon, majestic dancers who danced in her imagination; she would be enraptured before them with no time passing at all. My mother was in love with the ballet, and as there was no theatre in the village, those shelves must have been the nearest thing to sitting in a stall.

My friend looked at them a long while, took a pair and set them on the table; the rest of us, all but hypnotised, did the same until the shelves were bare. We set them out one after another, using boxes, plates, glasses and cutlery, shaping that winding course of several heights.

We tied the first of the figures to a string. Expectant. The seven of us held the string that with one movement brought down the first piece. And so it was that one by one the thirty and more ballerinas were smashed to pieces. Our faces were full of smiles, accompanied by a loud ovation, an ecstasy that lasted as long as it took them to eat all the chocolate left in the larder and vanish out of the door, taking their leave with a brief “see you tomorrow”, leaving me alone and with no notion how to undo the wrong.

Good sense came over me. I do not know what moved me to allow it, but the fact is that we all enjoyed it. And though my only wish was to mend the figures, return them to their place and have my mother never suspect a thing, I understood that I was unable to put right such a piece of work, even with good glue. So I stayed sitting at the table, gathering together the pieces of each figure, waiting for my mother to come back.

And it was not long before the door opened and a scream was heard at the other end of the village. It was something like this: “OOOOOOOHHHHH… LORENA… THE MOTHER THAT BORE YOU… I’LL KILL YOU!!!” Afterwards there was an everlasting silence.

― Go to your room before I skin you… -she said, looking at the floor. After such a threat, I had no choice but to go to my bedroom.

My mother was a mild woman, understanding; she never grew angry, and far less did she ever raise a hand to me. But I was certain that had I stayed there a second longer, I should have felt her wrath in my own flesh. After I had shut the door of my room, there was one last shout.

― MARCO ANTONIO!!!

So great was the outcry that several of the neighbouring women came running in alarm. My mother was weeping, her eyes bloodshot, her jaw slack. A few minutes later my father arrived.
